ARQUIVO 3/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/vite.config.ts
================================================================
import path from 'path';
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react';
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite';
import { defineConfig } from 'vite';

import runtimeErrorOverlay from '@replit/vite-plugin-runtime-error-modal';

const rawPort = process.env.PORT;

if (!rawPort) {
  throw new Error(
    'PORT environment variable is required but was not provided.',
  );
}

const port = Number(rawPort);

if (Number.isNaN(port) || port <= 0) {
  throw new Error(`Invalid PORT value: "${rawPort}"`);
}

const basePath = process.env.BASE_PATH;

if (!basePath) {
  throw new Error(
    'BASE_PATH environment variable is required but was not provided.',
  );
}

export default defineConfig({
  base: basePath,
  plugins: [
    react(),
    tailwindcss(),
    runtimeErrorOverlay(),
    ...(process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production' &&
    process.env.REPL_ID !== undefined
      ? [
          await import('@replit/vite-plugin-cartographer').then((m) =>
            m.cartographer({
              root: path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, '..'),
            }),
          ),
          await import('@replit/vite-plugin-dev-banner').then((m) =>
            m.devBanner(),
          ),
        ]
      : []),
  ],
  resolve: {
    alias: {
      '@': path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, 'src'),
      '@assets': path.resolve(
        import.meta.dirname,
        '..',
        '..',
        'attached_assets',
      ),
    },
    dedupe: ['react', 'react-dom'],
  },
  root: path.resolve(import.meta.dirname),
  build: {
    outDir: path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, 'dist/public'),
    emptyOutDir: true,
  },
  server: {
    port,
    strictPort: true,
    host: '0.0.0.0',
    allowedHosts: true,
    fs: {
      strict: true,
    },
  },
  preview: {
    port,
    host: '0.0.0.0',
    allowedHosts: true,
  },
});

import App from './App';
import { ErrorBoundary } from '@/components/error-boundary';

import './index.css';

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!, {
  // Keeps caught errors off reportError(), which would raise the dev overlay.
  onCaughtError: (error, errorInfo) => {
    console.error(error, errorInfo.componentStack);
  },
}).render(
  <ErrorBoundary>
    <App />
  </ErrorBoundary>,
);

ARQUIVO 6/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/App.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { ErrorBoundary } from "@/components/error-boundary";
import { Toaster } from "@/components/ui/toaster";
import { TooltipProvider } from "@/components/ui/tooltip";
import { AppShell } from "@/components/AppShell";
import { useAppData } from "@/app/use-app-data";
import {
  getAllHabitViews,
  getContextualMessage,
  getGoalViews,
  getStreaks,
  getTodayHabits,
  getTodaySummary,
} from "@/app/view-models";
import type { HabitView } from "@/app/view-models";
import type { StateChangeResult } from "@/app/app-data-controller";
import type {
  AppState,
  GoalSummary,
  HabitStatus,
} from "@/domain/model";
import { buildXpLedger, getLevelProgress } from "@/domain/progression";
import { OFFICIAL_ACHIEVEMENTS, OFFICIAL_PROGRESSION_RULES } from "@/domain/official-rules";
import NotFound from "@/pages/not-found";
import {
  AchievementsPage,
  DashboardPage,
  GoalsPage,
  HabitsPage,
  HistoryPage,
  SettingsPage,
} from "@/pages/Pages";
import {
  Route,
  Switch,
  useLocation,
  Link,
  Router as WouterRouter,
} from "wouter";
import { toLocalDateKey } from "@/domain/dates";
import type { HabitInput } from "@/domain/habits";
import type { GoalInput } from "@/domain/finance";
import type { GoalContribution } from "@/domain/model";
import { ArrowUpFromLine, Award, Leaf, X } from "lucide-react";

type ModalState =
  | { kind: "habit"; initial?: HabitView }
  | { kind: "goal"; initial?: GoalSummary }
  | { kind: "contribution"; goal: GoalSummary }
  | { kind: "correction"; contribution: GoalContribution; currency: string }
  | { kind: "justification"; habit: HabitView }
  | null;

const achievementCatalog = OFFICIAL_ACHIEVEMENTS;
const progressionRules = OFFICIAL_PROGRESSION_RULES;

function Modal({
  state,
  onClose,
  onSaveHabit,
  onSaveGoal,
  onContribute,
  onCorrect,
  onJustify,
}: {
  state: ModalState;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSaveHabit: (input: HabitInput, initial?: HabitView) => StateChangeResult;
  onSaveGoal: (input: GoalInput, initial?: GoalSummary) => StateChangeResult;
  onContribute: (goalId: string, amountCents: number, note: string) => StateChangeResult;
  onCorrect: (contributionId: string, amountCents: number, note: string) => StateChangeResult;
  onJustify: (habitId: string, context: string) => StateChangeResult;
}) {
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  if (!state) return null;

  const finish = (result: StateChangeResult) => {
    if (result.ok) onClose();
    else setFormError(result.error);
  };
  const submitHabit = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const weekdays = form.getAll("weekdays").map(Number);
    if (weekdays.length === 0) {
      setFormError("Selecione ao menos um dia da semana.");
      return;
    }
    const mainValue = Number(form.get("mainGoalValue") ?? "");
    const mainUnit = String(form.get("mainGoalUnit") ?? "").trim();
    const minValue = Number(form.get("minGoalValue") ?? "");
    const minUnit = String(form.get("minGoalUnit") ?? "").trim();
    const mainGoal = Number.isFinite(mainValue) && mainValue > 0 && mainUnit
      ? { value: mainValue, unit: mainUnit }
      : undefined;
    const minGoal = Number.isFinite(minValue) && minValue > 0 && minUnit
      ? { value: minValue, unit: minUnit }
      : undefined;
    finish(onSaveHabit({
      name: String(form.get("name") ?? ""),
      description: String(form.get("description") ?? ""),
      weekdays,
      mainGoal,
      minGoal,
    }, state.kind === "habit" ? state.initial : undefined));
  };
  const submitGoal = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const target = Number(form.get("target"));
    if (!Number.isFinite(target) || target <= 0) {
      setFormError("Informe um valor maior que zero.");
      return;
    }
    finish(onSaveGoal({
      name: String(form.get("name") ?? ""),
      description: String(form.get("description") ?? ""),
      targetCents: Math.round(target * 100),
    }, state.kind === "goal" ? state.initial : undefined));
  };
  const submitContribution = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const amount = Number(form.get("amount"));
    if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      setFormError("Informe um valor de aporte maior que zero.");
      return;
    }
    finish(onContribute(
      state.kind === "contribution" ? state.goal.goal.id : "",
      Math.round(amount * 100),
      String(form.get("note") ?? ""),
    ));
  };
  const submitCorrection = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const amount = Number(form.get("amount"));
    if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      setFormError("Informe o valor corrigido em reais.");
      return;
    }
    finish(onCorrect(
      state.kind === "correction" ? state.contribution.id : "",
      Math.round(amount * 100),
      String(form.get("note") ?? ""),
    ));
  };
  const submitJustification = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setFormError(null);
    const form = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    finish(onJustify(
      state.kind === "justification" ? state.habit.id : "",
      String(form.get("context") ?? ""),
    ));
  };

  const title = state.kind === "habit"
    ? state.initial ? "Editar hábito" : "Criar hábito"
    : state.kind === "goal"
      ? state.initial ? "Editar meta" : "Criar meta financeira"
      : state.kind === "contribution"
        ? `Aporte para ${state.goal.goal.name}`
        : state.kind === "correction"
          ? "Corrigir aporte"
          : `Justificar ${state.habit.name}`;
  const description = state.kind === "habit"
    ? "Escolha o nome e os dias em que este hábito faz parte da sua rotina."
    : state.kind === "goal"
      ? "Defina um objetivo e um valor em reais."
      : state.kind === "contribution"
        ? `Registre um aporte para a meta “${state.goal.goal.name}”.`
        : state.kind === "correction"
          ? "A correção preserva o valor anterior no histórico."
          : "A justificativa fica registrada e não gera XP.";

  return (
    <div
      className="modal-backdrop"
      role="presentation"
      onMouseDown={(event) => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose(); }}
      onKeyDown={(event) => { if (event.key === "Escape") onClose(); }}
    >
      <section className="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="modal-title">
        <button type="button" className="modal-close icon-button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Fechar janela"><X aria-hidden="true" /></button>
        <h2 id="modal-title">{title}</h2>
        <p>{description}</p>
        {state.kind === "habit" && (
          <form className="form-grid" onSubmit={submitHabit}>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="habit-name">Nome do hábito</label>
              <input className="input" id="habit-name" name="name" required maxLength={80} defaultValue={state.initial?.name ?? ""} placeholder="Ex.: caminhar no parque" autoFocus data-testid="input-habit-name" />
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="habit-description">Descrição (opcional)</label>
              <textarea className="textarea" id="habit-description" name="description" maxLength={500} defaultValue={state.initial?.description ?? ""} placeholder="Um contexto para você lembrar depois" data-testid="input-habit-description" />
            </div>
            <fieldset className="field weekday-field">
              <legend>Dias da semana</legend>
              <div className="weekday-options">
                {[
                  ["0", "Dom"], ["1", "Seg"], ["2", "Ter"], ["3", "Qua"],
                  ["4", "Qui"], ["5", "Sex"], ["6", "Sáb"],
                ].map(([value, label]) => (
                  <label className="weekday-option" key={value}>
                    <input type="checkbox" name="weekdays" value={value} defaultChecked={state.initial ? state.initial.weekdays.includes(Number(value)) : true} data-testid={`input-habit-weekday-${value}`} />
                    <span>{label}</span>
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
            </fieldset>
            <div className="goal-fields">
              <div className="field">
                <label htmlFor="habit-main-value">Meta principal (opcional)</label>
                <div className="goal-input-row">
                  <input className="input" id="habit-main-value" name="mainGoalValue" type="number" min="0" step="any" defaultValue={state.initial?.mainGoal?.value ?? ""} placeholder="30" data-testid="input-habit-main-value" />
                  <input className="input" name="mainGoalUnit" maxLength={40} defaultValue={state.initial?.mainGoal?.unit ?? ""} placeholder="min" data-testid="input-habit-main-unit" />
                </div>
              </div>
              <div className="field">
                <label htmlFor="habit-min-value">Meta mínima (opcional)</label>
                <div className="goal-input-row">
                  <input className="input" id="habit-min-value" name="minGoalValue" type="number" min="0" step="any" defaultValue={state.initial?.minGoal?.value ?? ""} placeholder="5" data-testid="input-habit-min-value" />
                  <input className="input" name="minGoalUnit" maxLength={40} defaultValue={state.initial?.minGoal?.unit ?? ""} placeholder="min" data-testid="input-habit-min-unit" />
                </div>
                <span className="field-hint">A meta mínima é uma versão reduzida para dias difíceis.</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <ModalActions onClose={onClose} submitLabel="Salvar hábito" />
          </form>
        )}
        {state.kind === "goal" && (
          <form className="form-grid" onSubmit={submitGoal}>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="goal-name">Nome da meta</label>
              <input className="input" id="goal-name" name="name" required maxLength={100} defaultValue={state.initial?.goal.name ?? ""} autoFocus data-testid="input-goal-name" />
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="goal-description">Descrição (opcional)</label>
              <textarea className="textarea" id="goal-description" name="description" maxLength={500} defaultValue={state.initial?.goal.description ?? ""} data-testid="input-goal-description" />
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="goal-target">Valor desejado (R$)</label>
              <input className="input" id="goal-target" name="target" type="number" min="0.01" step="0.01" required defaultValue={state.initial ? (state.initial.goal.targetCents / 100).toFixed(2) : ""} placeholder="0,00" data-testid="input-goal-target" />
            </div>
            <ModalActions onClose={onClose} submitLabel="Salvar meta" />
          </form>
        )}
        {state.kind === "contribution" && (
          <form className="form-grid" onSubmit={submitContribution}>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="contribution-amount">Valor do aporte (R$)</label>
              <input className="input" id="contribution-amount" name="amount" type="number" min="0.01" step="0.01" required autoFocus placeholder="0,00" data-testid="input-contribution-amount" />
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="contribution-note">Observação (opcional)</label>
              <input className="input" id="contribution-note" name="note" maxLength={500} data-testid="input-contribution-note" />
            </div>
            <ModalActions onClose={onClose} submitLabel="Registrar aporte" />
          </form>
        )}
        {state.kind === "correction" && (
          <form className="form-grid" onSubmit={submitCorrection}>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="correction-amount">Valor corrigido (R$)</label>
              <input className="input" id="correction-amount" name="amount" type="number" min="0.01" step="0.01" required defaultValue={(state.contribution.amountCents / 100).toFixed(2)} autoFocus data-testid="input-correction-amount" />
            </div>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="correction-note">Motivo da correção (opcional)</label>
              <textarea className="textarea" id="correction-note" name="note" maxLength={500} data-testid="input-correction-note" />
            </div>
            <ModalActions onClose={onClose} submitLabel="Salvar correção" />
          </form>
        )}
        {state.kind === "justification" && (
          <form className="form-grid" onSubmit={submitJustification}>
            <div className="field">
              <label htmlFor="justification-context">Contexto da justificativa</label>
              <textarea className="textarea" id="justification-context" name="context" required maxLength={2000} minLength={1} autoFocus data-testid="input-justification-context" />
              <span className="field-hint">Até duas justificativas em uma janela móvel de sete dias. Justificar não concede XP.</span>
            </div>
            <ModalActions onClose={onClose} submitLabel="Salvar justificativa" />
          </form>
        )}
        {formError && <p className="inline-message error-message" role="alert" data-testid="text-form-error">{formError}</p>}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

function ModalActions({ onClose, submitLabel }: { onClose: () => void; submitLabel: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="modal-actions">
      <button type="button" className="button button-quiet" onClick={onClose} data-testid="button-cancel-modal">Cancelar</button>
      <button className="button button-primary" type="submit" data-testid="button-save-modal">{submitLabel}</button>
    </div>
  );
}

function Router() {
  const [location] = useLocation();
  const { state, error, controller, newlyUnlockedAchievementIds } = useAppData();
  const [modal, setModal] = useState<ModalState>(null);
  const today = toLocalDateKey();

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!state) return;
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("dark", state.preferences.theme === "dark");
    document.documentElement.classList.toggle("reduce-motion", state.preferences.reducedMotion);
    document.documentElement.lang = "pt-BR";
  }, [state?.preferences.theme, state?.preferences.reducedMotion, state]);

  const allHabits = useMemo(
    () => state ? getAllHabitViews(state, today) : [],
    [state, today],
  );
  const todayHabits = useMemo(
    () => state ? getTodayHabits(state, today) : [],
    [state, today],
  );
  const goals = useMemo(
    () => state ? getGoalViews(state) : [],
    [state],
  );
  const dailySummary = state ? getTodaySummary(state, today) : null;
  const streaks = state ? getStreaks(state, today) : null;
  const message = state ? getContextualMessage(state, today) : "";
  const progression = state && progressionRules
    ? buildXpLedger(state, progressionRules)
    : null;
  const xpTotal = progression
    ? progression.reduce((total, entry) => total + entry.amountXp, 0)
    : null;
  const level = xpTotal !== null && progressionRules
    ? getLevelProgress(xpTotal, progressionRules.levels)
    : null;

  const saveHabit = (input: HabitInput, initial?: HabitView): StateChangeResult =>
    initial
      ? controller.updateHabit(initial.id, input, today)
      : controller.createHabit(input, today);
  const setHabitStatus = (habitId: string, status: HabitStatus, context?: string, level?: "main" | "min") =>
    controller.setHabitStatus(habitId, today, status, context, level);
  const pauseHabit = (habitId: string, paused: boolean) =>
    controller.setHabitPaused(habitId, paused, today);
  const deleteHabit = (habit: HabitView) => {
    if (window.confirm(`Excluir “${habit.name}” da rotina? O histórico anterior será preservado.`)) {
      controller.deleteHabit(habit.id, today);
    }
  };
  const saveGoal = (input: GoalInput, initial?: GoalSummary) =>
    initial
      ? controller.updateGoal(initial.goal.id, input)
      : controller.createGoal(input, today);
  const addContribution = (goalId: string, amountCents: number, note: string) =>
    controller.addContribution({ goalId, amountCents, note, date: today });
  const correctContribution = (contributionId: string, amountCents: number, note: string) =>
    controller.correctContribution(contributionId, amountCents, note);
  const archiveGoal = (goal: GoalSummary) => {
    if (window.confirm(`Arquivar a meta “${goal.goal.name}”? Os aportes e o histórico serão preservados.`)) {
      controller.archiveGoal(goal.goal.id, today);
    }
  };
  const exportBackup = () => {
    try {
      const blob = new Blob([controller.exportBackup()], { type: "application/json" });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const anchor = document.createElement("a");
      anchor.href = url;
      anchor.download = `passo-a-passo-backup-${today}.json`;
      anchor.click();
      window.setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1000);
    } catch (exportError) {
      window.alert(exportError instanceof Error ? exportError.message : "Não foi possível exportar o backup.");
    }
  };
  const importBackup = async (file: File): Promise<string | null> => {
    if (state && hasSavedData(state) && !window.confirm("Restaurar este backup substituirá os dados atuais deste dispositivo. Deseja continuar?")) {
      return "Importação cancelada.";
    }
    try {
      const result = controller.importBackup(await file.text());
      return result.ok ? null : result.error;
    } catch (importError) {
      return importError instanceof Error ? importError.message : "Não foi possível ler o arquivo.";
    }
  };
  const clearData = () => {
    if (window.confirm("Apagar todos os hábitos, registros, metas e preferências deste dispositivo? Esta ação não pode ser desfeita sem um backup.")) {
      controller.clearAllData();
    }
  };

  if (!state) {
    return (
      <RecoveryScreen
        error={error}
        onImport={importBackup}
        onClear={() => {
          if (window.confirm("Remover os dados locais corrompidos? Faça isso apenas se não tiver um backup para restaurar.")) {
            controller.clearAllData();
          }
        }}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <ErrorBoundary resetKey={location}>
      <AppShell>
        {error && (
          <div className="global-error" role="alert" data-testid="status-storage-error">
            <span>{error}</span>
            <button type="button" className="icon-button" onClick={() => controller.load()} aria-label="Tentar novamente"><ArrowUpFromLine aria-hidden="true" /></button>
          </div>
        )}
        {newlyUnlockedAchievementIds.length > 0 && (
          <div className="achievement-announcement" role="status" data-testid="announcement-achievement">
            <span className="achievement-announcement-icon"><Award aria-hidden="true" /></span>
            <span className="achievement-announcement-copy">
              <strong>{newlyUnlockedAchievementIds.length === 1 ? "Conquista desbloqueada" : "Conquistas desbloqueadas"}</strong>
              <small>{newlyUnlockedAchievementIds.map((id) => achievementCatalog.find((item) => item.id === id)?.name ?? id).join(", ")}</small>
            </span>
            <Link href="/achievements" className="achievement-announcement-link">Ver conquistas</Link>
            <button type="button" className="icon-button" onClick={() => controller.dismissAchievementNotifications()} aria-label="Dispensar aviso"><X aria-hidden="true" /></button>
          </div>
        )}
        <Switch>
          <Route path="/" component={() => (
            <DashboardPage
              state={state}
              today={today}
              habits={todayHabits}
              summary={dailySummary!}
              streaks={streaks!}
              goals={goals}
              message={message}
              level={level}
              xpTotal={xpTotal}
              achievements={achievementCatalog}
              onAddHabit={() => setModal({ kind: "habit" })}
              onSetStatus={(id, status, level) => setHabitStatus(id, status, undefined, level)}
              onJustify={(habit) => setModal({ kind: "justification", habit })}
            />
          )} />
          <Route path="/habits" component={() => (
            <HabitsPage
              habits={allHabits}
              onAddHabit={() => setModal({ kind: "habit" })}
              onEditHabit={(habit) => setModal({ kind: "habit", initial: habit })}
              onSetStatus={(id, status, level) => setHabitStatus(id, status, undefined, level)}
              onJustify={(habit) => setModal({ kind: "justification", habit })}
              onPauseHabit={pauseHabit}
              onDeleteHabit={deleteHabit}
            />
          )} />
          <Route path="/history" component={() => <HistoryPage state={state} />} />
          <Route path="/achievements" component={() => <AchievementsPage state={state} catalog={achievementCatalog} />} />
          <Route path="/goals" component={() => (
            <GoalsPage
              state={state}
              goals={goals}
              onCreateGoal={() => setModal({ kind: "goal" })}
              onEditGoal={(goal) => setModal({ kind: "goal", initial: goal })}
              onAddContribution={(goal) => setModal({ kind: "contribution", goal })}
              onCorrectContribution={(goal, id) => {
                const contribution = goal.contributions.find((item) => item.id === id);
                if (contribution) setModal({ kind: "correction", contribution, currency: state.preferences.currency });
              }}
              onArchiveGoal={archiveGoal}
            />
          )} />
          <Route path="/settings" component={() => (
            <SettingsPage
              preferences={state.preferences}
              error={error}
              onUpdate={(patch) => { controller.updatePreferences(patch); }}
              onExport={exportBackup}
              onImport={importBackup}
              onClearData={clearData}
            />
          )} />
          <Route component={NotFound} />
        </Switch>
      </AppShell>
      <Modal
        state={modal}
        onClose={() => setModal(null)}
        onSaveHabit={saveHabit}
        onSaveGoal={saveGoal}
        onContribute={addContribution}
        onCorrect={correctContribution}
        onJustify={(habitId, context) => setHabitStatus(habitId, "justified", context)}
      />
    </ErrorBoundary>
  );
}

function RecoveryScreen({
  error,
  onImport,
  onClear,
}: {
  error: string | null;
  onImport: (file: File) => Promise<string | null>;
  onClear: () => void;
}) {
  const [message, setMessage] = useState(error);
  const restore = async (file?: File) => {
    if (!file) return;
    const result = await onImport(file);
    setMessage(result ?? "Backup restaurado e validado.");
  };
  return (
    <main className="recovery-screen">
      <div className="recovery-card">
        <span className="brand-mark"><Leaf aria-hidden="true" /></span>
        <h1>Não foi possível carregar os dados</h1>
        <p>Os dados salvos não foram substituídos. Você pode restaurar um backup válido ou remover o armazenamento local.</p>
        {message && <p role="alert" className="inline-message error-message">{message}</p>}
        <label className="button button-primary" htmlFor="recovery-file">
          Restaurar backup JSON
          <input id="recovery-file" type="file" accept="application/json,.json" hidden onChange={(event) => void restore(event.target.files?.[0])} />
        </label>
        <button type="button" className="button button-danger" onClick={onClear}>Remover dados locais</button>
      </div>
    </main>
  );
}

function hasSavedData(state: AppState): boolean {
  return state.habits.length > 0 ||
    state.habitDays.length > 0 ||
    state.goals.length > 0 ||
    state.contributions.length > 0 ||
    state.achievementUnlocks.length > 0;
}

function App() {
  return (
    <TooltipProvider>
      <WouterRouter base={import.meta.env.BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "")}>
        <Router />
      </WouterRouter>
      <Toaster />
    </TooltipProvider>
  );
}

export default App;


================================================================
ARQUIVO 7/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/index.css
================================================================
@import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=DM+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Manrope:wght@500;600;700;800&display=swap');

/**
 * Using the elevate system.
 * Automatic contrast adjustment.
 *
 * <element className="hover-elevate" />
 * <element className="active-elevate-2" />
 *
 * // Using the tailwind utility when a data attribute is "on"
 * <element className="toggle-elevate data-[state=on]:toggle-elevated" />
 * // Or manually controlling the toggle state
 * <element className="toggle-elevate toggle-elevated" />
 *
 * Elevation systems have to handle many states.
 * - not-hovered, vs. hovered vs. active  (three mutually exclusive states)
 * - toggled or not
 * - focused or not (this is not handled with these utilities)
 *
 * Even without handling focused or not, this is six possible combinations that
 * need to be distinguished from eachother visually.
 */
@layer utilities {
  /* Hide ugly search cancel button in Chrome until we can style it properly */
  input[type='search']::-webkit-search-cancel-button {
    @apply hidden;
  }

  /* Placeholder styling for contentEditable div */
  [contenteditable][data-placeholder]:empty::before {
    content: attr(data-placeholder);
    color: hsl(var(--muted-foreground));
    pointer-events: none;
  }

  /* .no-default-hover-elevate/no-default-active-elevate is an escape hatch so consumers of
   * buttons/badges can remove the automatic brightness adjustment on interactions
   * and program their own. */
  .no-default-hover-elevate {
  }

  .no-default-active-elevate {
  }

  /**
   * Toggleable backgrounds go behind the content. Hoverable/active goes on top.
   * This way they can stack/compound. Both will overlap the parent's borders!
   * So borders will be automatically adjusted both on toggle, and hover/active,
   * and they will be compounded.
   */
  .toggle-elevate::before,
  .toggle-elevate-2::before {
    content: '';
    pointer-events: none;
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0px;
    /*border-radius: inherit;   match rounded corners */
    border-radius: inherit;
    z-index: -1;
    /* sits behind content but above backdrop */
  }

  .toggle-elevate.toggle-elevated::before {
    background-color: var(--elevate-2);
  }

  /* If there's a 1px border, adjust the inset so that it covers that parent's border */
  .border.toggle-elevate::before {
    inset: -1px;
  }

  /* Does not work on elements with overflow:hidden! */
  .hover-elevate:not(.no-default-hover-elevate),
  .active-elevate:not(.no-default-active-elevate),
  .hover-elevate-2:not(.no-default-hover-elevate),
  .active-elevate-2:not(.no-default-active-elevate) {
    position: relative;
    z-index: 0;
  }

  .hover-elevate:not(.no-default-hover-elevate)::after,
  .active-elevate:not(.no-default-active-elevate)::after,
  .hover-elevate-2:not(.no-default-hover-elevate)::after,
  .active-elevate-2:not(.no-default-active-elevate)::after {
    content: '';
    pointer-events: none;
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0px;
    /*border-radius: inherit;   match rounded corners */
    border-radius: inherit;
    z-index: 999;
    /* sits in front of content */
  }

  .hover-elevate:hover:not(.no-default-hover-elevate)::after,
  .active-elevate:active:not(.no-default-active-elevate)::after {
    background-color: var(--elevate-1);
  }

  .hover-elevate-2:hover:not(.no-default-hover-elevate)::after,
  .active-elevate-2:active:not(.no-default-active-elevate)::after {
    background-color: var(--elevate-2);
  }

  /* If there's a 1px border, adjust the inset so that it covers that parent's border */
  .border.hover-elevate:not(.no-hover-interaction-elevate)::after,
  .border.active-elevate:not(.no-active-interaction-elevate)::after,
  .border.hover-elevate-2:not(.no-hover-interaction-elevate)::after,
  .border.active-elevate-2:not(.no-active-interaction-elevate)::after,
  .border.hover-elevate:not(.no-hover-interaction-elevate)::after {
    inset: -1px;
  }
}

ARQUIVO 10/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/app/app-data-controller.ts

import { toLocalDateKey } from "../domain/dates.ts";
import { addContribution, archiveGoal, correctContribution, createGoal, listGoalSummaries, updateGoal, type ContributionInput, type GoalInput } from "../domain/finance.ts";
import { createHabit, recordHabitStatus, setHabitLifecycle, updateHabit, type HabitInput } from "../domain/habits.ts";
import { createId, createInitialState } from "../domain/state.ts";
import type { AppPreferences, AppState, HabitLifecycleStatus, HabitStatus } from "../domain/model.ts";
import { OFFICIAL_ACHIEVEMENTS, OFFICIAL_PROGRESSION_RULES } from "../domain/official-rules.ts";
import { unlockEligibleAchievements } from "../domain/progression.ts";
import { LocalStore } from "../persistence/local-store.ts";
import type { StoragePort } from "../persistence/local-store.ts";

export interface AppDataSnapshot {
  state: AppState | null;
  error: string | null;
  revision: number;
  newlyUnlockedAchievementIds: string[];
}

type Listener = () => void;
export type StateChangeResult = { ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string };

export class AppDataController {
  private snapshot: AppDataSnapshot = {
    state: null,
    error: null,
    revision: 0,
    newlyUnlockedAchievementIds: [],
  };
  private listeners = new Set<Listener>();
  private readonly store: LocalStore;

  constructor(storage: StoragePort) {
    this.store = new LocalStore(storage);
  }

  readonly subscribe = (listener: Listener): (() => void) => {
    this.listeners.add(listener);
    return () => this.listeners.delete(listener);
  };

  readonly getSnapshot = (): AppDataSnapshot => this.snapshot;

  load(): void {
    try {
      this.snapshot = {
        state: this.store.load(),
        error: null,
        revision: this.snapshot.revision + 1,
        newlyUnlockedAchievementIds: [],
      };
    } catch (error) {
      this.snapshot = {
        ...this.snapshot,
        error: messageOf(error),
      };
    }
    this.emit();
  }

  createHabit(input: HabitInput, date = toLocalDateKey()): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) => createHabit(state, input, date, createId));
  }

  updateHabit(
    habitId: string,
    input: HabitInput,
    effectiveFrom = toLocalDateKey(),
  ): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) =>
      updateHabit(state, habitId, input, effectiveFrom, createId),
    );
  }

  setHabitPaused(
    habitId: string,
    paused: boolean,
    effectiveFrom = toLocalDateKey(),
  ): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) =>
      setHabitLifecycle(
        state,
        habitId,
        paused ? "paused" : "active",
        effectiveFrom,
        createId,
      ),
    );
  }

  deleteHabit(habitId: string, effectiveFrom = toLocalDateKey()): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) =>
      setHabitLifecycle(state, habitId, "archived", effectiveFrom, createId),
    );
  }

  setHabitStatus(
    habitId: string,
    date: string,
    status: HabitStatus,
    context?: string,
    level?: "main" | "min",
    now = new Date(),
  ): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) =>
      recordHabitStatus(state, habitId, date, status, context, now, createId, level),
    );
  }

  createGoal(input: GoalInput, date = toLocalDateKey()): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) => createGoal(state, input, date, createId));
  }

  updateGoal(goalId: string, input: GoalInput): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) => updateGoal(state, goalId, input));
  }

  archiveGoal(goalId: string, date = toLocalDateKey()): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) => archiveGoal(state, goalId, date));
  }

  addContribution(
    input: ContributionInput,
    now = new Date(),
  ): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) => addContribution(state, input, now, createId));
  }

  correctContribution(
    contributionId: string,
    amountCents: number,
    note: string,
    now = new Date(),
  ): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) =>
      correctContribution(state, contributionId, amountCents, note, now),
    );
  }

  updatePreferences(
    patch: Partial<AppPreferences>,
  ): StateChangeResult {
    return this.change((state) => ({
      ...state,
      preferences: { ...state.preferences, ...patch },
    }));
  }

  exportBackup(): string {
    const state = this.requireState();
    return this.store.exportBackup(state);
  }

  importBackup(json: string): StateChangeResult {
    try {
      const state = this.store.importBackup(json);
      this.snapshot = {
        state,
        error: null,
        revision: this.snapshot.revision + 1,
        newlyUnlockedAchievementIds: [],
      };
      this.emit();
      return { ok: true };
    } catch (error) {
      const message = messageOf(error);
      this.setError(message);
      return { ok: false, error: message };
    }
  }

  clearAllData(): StateChangeResult {
    try {
      const state = createInitialState();
      this.store.save(state);
      this.snapshot = {
        state,
        error: null,
        revision: this.snapshot.revision + 1,
        newlyUnlockedAchievementIds: [],
      };
      this.emit();
      return { ok: true };
    } catch (error) {
      const message = messageOf(error);
      this.setError(message);
      return { ok: false, error: message };
    }
  }

  getActiveGoalSummaries() {
    const state = this.snapshot.state;
    return state ? listGoalSummaries(state) : [];
  }

  private change(updater: (state: AppState) => AppState): StateChangeResult {
    try {
      const current = this.requireState();
      let next = updater(current);
      if (next === current) return { ok: true };
      if (OFFICIAL_PROGRESSION_RULES) {
        next = unlockEligibleAchievements(
          next,
          OFFICIAL_ACHIEVEMENTS,
          OFFICIAL_PROGRESSION_RULES,
          toLocalDateKey(),
        );
      }
      this.store.save(next);
      const previousUnlocked = new Set(
        current.achievementUnlocks.map((item) => item.achievementId),
      );
      const newlyUnlockedAchievementIds = next.achievementUnlocks
        .filter((item) => !previousUnlocked.has(item.achievementId))
        .map((item) => item.achievementId);
      this.snapshot = {
        state: next,
        error: null,
        revision: this.snapshot.revision + 1,
        newlyUnlockedAchievementIds,
      };
      this.emit();
      return { ok: true };
    } catch (error) {
      const message = messageOf(error);
      this.setError(message);
      return { ok: false, error: message };
    }
  }

  private requireState(): AppState {
    if (!this.snapshot.state) {
      throw new Error("Os dados ainda não foram carregados.");
    }
    return this.snapshot.state;
  }

  private setError(error: string): void {
    this.snapshot = { ...this.snapshot, error };
    this.emit();
  }

  dismissAchievementNotifications(): void {
    if (this.snapshot.newlyUnlockedAchievementIds.length === 0) return;
    this.snapshot = { ...this.snapshot, newlyUnlockedAchievementIds: [] };
    this.emit();
  }

  private emit(): void {
    for (const listener of this.listeners) listener();
  }
}

export function getLifecycleStatus(state: AppState, habitId: string, date: string) {
  const habit = state.habits.find((item) => item.id === habitId);
  if (!habit) return undefined;
  let latest: HabitLifecycleStatus | undefined;
  for (const event of habit.lifecycle) {
    if (event.effectiveFrom <= date) latest = event.status;
  }
  return latest;
}

function messageOf(error: unknown): string {
  return error instanceof Error ? error.message : "Ocorreu um erro inesperado.";
}

ARQUIVO 11/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/app/use-app-data.ts
================================================================
import { useEffect, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { AppDataController } from "./app-data-controller";

function createController(): AppDataController {
  return new AppDataController({
    getItem: (key) => window.localStorage.getItem(key),
    setItem: (key, value) => window.localStorage.setItem(key, value),
    removeItem: (key) => window.localStorage.removeItem(key),
  });
}

export function useAppData() {
  const [controller] = useState(createController);
  const snapshot = useSyncExternalStore(
    controller.subscribe,
    controller.getSnapshot,
    controller.getSnapshot,
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    controller.load();
  }, [controller]);

  return { ...snapshot, controller };
}

ARQUIVO 12/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/app/view-models.ts

import { addLocalDays, compareLocalDates, daysBetween, toLocalDateKey, weekdayForDate } from "../domain/dates.ts";
import { formatCents, listGoalSummaries } from "../domain/finance.ts";
import {
  getDailySummary,
  getHabitDayEntry,
  getHabitRevision,
  getHabitStatus,
  isHabitScheduledOn,
} from "../domain/habits.ts";
import { getStreakSummary } from "../domain/progression.ts";
import type { AppState, GoalSpec, Habit, HabitLifecycleStatus, HabitStatus, LocalDate } from "../domain/model.ts";

const WEEKDAY_LABELS = ["dom", "seg", "ter", "qua", "qui", "sex", "sáb"];

export interface HabitView {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  status: HabitLifecycleStatus;
  weekdays: number[];
  frequency: string;
  scheduledToday: boolean;
  dayStatus: HabitStatus;
  context?: string;
  mainGoal?: GoalSpec;
  minGoal?: GoalSpec;
  achievedLevel?: "main" | "min";
  completionCount: number;
  justificationCount: number;
}

export interface DayView {
  date: LocalDate;
  completed: number;
  justified: number;
  pending: number;
  neutral: number;
  scheduled: number;
  isComplete: boolean;
}

export interface MonthView {
  year: number;
  month: number;
  days: DayView[];
  completedHabits: number;
  justifiedHabits: number;
  completeDays: number;
}

export function getTodayHabits(state: AppState, today = toLocalDateKey()): HabitView[] {
  return getDailySummary(state, today).scheduled.map((scheduled) => {
    const entry = getHabitDayEntry(state, scheduled.habit.id, today);
    return toHabitView(state, scheduled.habit, today, scheduled.status, entry?.events.at(-1)?.context);
  });
}

export function getAllHabitViews(state: AppState, today = toLocalDateKey()): HabitView[] {
  return state.habits
    .filter((habit) => compareLocalDates(habit.createdOn, today) <= 0)
    .map((habit) => toHabitView(state, habit, today))
    .filter((view) => view.status !== "archived")
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name, "pt-BR"));
}

export function getHabitHistory(
  state: AppState,
  habitId: string,
): HabitDayEntryView[] {
  return state.habitDays
    .filter((entry) => entry.habitId === habitId)
    .map((entry) => {
      const event = entry.events.at(-1);
      const habit = state.habits.find((item) => item.id === entry.habitId);
      const revision = habit?.revisions.find((item) => item.id === entry.habitRevisionId);
      return {
        date: entry.date,
        name: revision?.name ?? "Hábito",
        status: event?.status ?? "pending",
        context: event?.context,
      };
    })
    .sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date));
}

export interface HabitDayEntryView {
  date: LocalDate;
  name: string;
  status: HabitStatus;
  context?: string;
}

export function getMonthView(
  state: AppState,
  year: number,
  monthIndex: number,
  habitId?: string,
): MonthView {
  const totalDays = new Date(year, monthIndex + 1, 0).getDate();
  let completedHabits = 0;
  let justifiedHabits = 0;
  let completeDays = 0;
  const days: DayView[] = [];
  for (let day = 1; day <= totalDays; day += 1) {
    const date = `${year}-${String(monthIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(day).padStart(2, "0")}`;
    const summary = getDailySummary(state, date);
    const matchingHabits = habitId
      ? summary.scheduled.filter((item) => item.habit.id === habitId)
      : summary.scheduled;
    const completed = matchingHabits.filter((item) => item.status === "completed").length;
    const justified = matchingHabits.filter((item) => item.status === "justified").length;
    const pending = matchingHabits.filter((item) => item.status === "pending").length;
    const neutral = matchingHabits.filter((item) => item.status === "neutral").length;
    const isComplete = matchingHabits.length > 0 && completed === matchingHabits.length;
    completedHabits += completed;
    justifiedHabits += justified;
    if (isComplete) completeDays += 1;
    days.push({
      date,
      completed,
      justified,
      pending,
      neutral,
      scheduled: matchingHabits.length,
      isComplete,
    });
  }
  return { year, month: monthIndex, days, completedHabits, justifiedHabits, completeDays };
}

export function getTodaySummary(state: AppState, today = toLocalDateKey()) {
  return getDailySummary(state, today);
}

export function getStreaks(state: AppState, today = toLocalDateKey()) {
  return getStreakSummary(state, today);
}

export function getActiveGoalView(state: AppState) {
  return listGoalSummaries(state)
    .filter((summary) => !summary.isComplete)
    .sort((a, b) => a.goal.createdOn.localeCompare(b.goal.createdOn))[0]
    ?? listGoalSummaries(state)[0];
}

export function getGoalViews(state: AppState) {
  return listGoalSummaries(state);
}

export function getContextualMessage(state: AppState, today = toLocalDateKey()): string {
  const summary = getDailySummary(state, today);
  if (summary.scheduled.length === 0) {
    return state.habits.length === 0
      ? "Comece com um hábito importante para você."
      : "Hoje não há hábitos programados. Aproveite o seu ritmo.";
  }
  const inactiveDays = getDaysSinceLastActivity(state, today);
  if (inactiveDays !== null && inactiveDays >= 3) {
    return "Você não precisa recuperar o tempo perdido. Só precisa dar o próximo passo.";
  }
  if (summary.isComplete) return "Você concluiu tudo o que planejou para hoje. Isso merece celebração.";
  if (summary.completed > 0) {
    const minGoals = countMinGoalsToday(state, today);
    if (minGoals > 0 && summary.completed <= minGoals) {
      return "Você fez algo por si mesmo hoje. Isso importa.";
    }
    return "Você já deu o primeiro passo. Continue no seu ritmo.";
  }
  if (summary.justified > 0) return "Seu progresso também inclui cuidar dos seus limites.";
  return "Cinco minutos também contam.";
}

function getDaysSinceLastActivity(state: AppState, today: LocalDate): number | null {
  const latest = state.habitDays
    .map((entry) => entry.events.at(-1))
    .filter((event): event is NonNullable<typeof event> => Boolean(event?.recordedOn))
    .sort((a, b) => b.recordedOn.localeCompare(a.recordedOn))[0];
  if (!latest) return null;
  return Math.max(0, daysBetween(latest.recordedOn, today));
}

function countMinGoalsToday(state: AppState, today: LocalDate): number {
  return state.habitDays.filter(
    (entry) =>
      entry.date === today &&
      entry.events.at(-1)?.status === "completed" &&
      entry.events.at(-1)?.level === "min",
  ).length;
}

export interface WeeklyReport {
  start: LocalDate;
  end: LocalDate;
  scheduledDays: number;
  completedDays: number;
  mainGoals: number;
  minGoals: number;
  totalCompleted: number;
  bestDay: LocalDate | null;
  suggestion: string;
}

export function getWeeklyReport(state: AppState, today = toLocalDateKey()): WeeklyReport {
  const end = today;
  const start = addLocalDays(today, -6);
  let scheduledDays = 0;
  let completedDays = 0;
  let mainGoals = 0;
  let minGoals = 0;
  let totalCompleted = 0;
  let bestDay: LocalDate | null = null;
  let bestCount = 0;
  for (let offset = 6; offset >= 0; offset -= 1) {
    const date = addLocalDays(today, -offset);
    const summary = getDailySummary(state, date);
    if (summary.scheduled.length > 0) scheduledDays += 1;
    if (summary.completed > 0) {
      completedDays += 1;
      totalCompleted += summary.completed;
      if (summary.completed > bestCount) {
        bestCount = summary.completed;
        bestDay = date;
      }
    }
    for (const item of summary.scheduled) {
      const event = getHabitDayEntry(state, item.habit.id, date)?.events.at(-1);
      if (event?.status === "completed") {
        if (event.level === "main") mainGoals += 1;
        else if (event.level === "min") minGoals += 1;
      }
    }
  }
  let suggestion = "Continue mantendo os hábitos que funcionaram esta semana.";
  if (totalCompleted === 0) suggestion = "Que tal começar com uma meta mínima pequena amanhã?";
  else if (mainGoals === 0 && minGoals > 0) suggestion = "Experimente ampliar uma das metas mínimas para a principal.";
  else if (scheduledDays > 0 && completedDays / scheduledDays < 0.5) suggestion = "Reduza temporariamente as metas para facilitar a constância.";
  return { start, end, scheduledDays, completedDays, mainGoals, minGoals, totalCompleted, bestDay, suggestion };
}

export function getGreeting(date = new Date()): string {
  const hour = date.getHours();
  if (hour < 12) return "Bom dia";
  if (hour < 18) return "Boa tarde";
  return "Boa noite";
}

export function formatLocalDate(date: LocalDate, options?: Intl.DateTimeFormatOptions): string {
  const [year, month, day] = date.split("-").map(Number);
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", options ?? {
    weekday: "long",
    day: "numeric",
    month: "long",
  }).format(new Date(year, month - 1, day));
}

export function formatWeekdays(weekdays: number[]): string {
  if (weekdays.length === 7) return "Todos os dias";
  if (weekdays.length === 5 && [1, 2, 3, 4, 5].every((day) => weekdays.includes(day))) {
    return "Dias úteis";
  }
  if (weekdays.length === 2 && [0, 6].every((day) => weekdays.includes(day))) {
    return "Fins de semana";
  }
  return weekdays
    .slice()
    .sort((a, b) => a - b)
    .map((day) => WEEKDAY_LABELS[day])
    .join(", ");
}

export function formatMoneyForState(state: AppState, cents: number): string {
  return formatCents(cents, state.preferences.currency);
}

function toHabitView(
  state: AppState,
  habit: Habit,
  date: LocalDate,
  knownStatus?: HabitStatus,
  knownContext?: string,
): HabitView {
  const revision = getHabitRevision(habit, date) ?? habit.revisions.at(-1)!;
  const entry = getHabitDayEntry(state, habit.id, date);
  const currentEvent = entry?.events.at(-1);
  const completed = state.habitDays.filter(
    (item) => item.habitId === habit.id && item.events.at(-1)?.status === "completed",
  ).length;
  const justified = state.habitDays.reduce(
    (total, item) =>
      item.habitId === habit.id
        ? total + item.events.filter((event) => event.status === "justified").length
        : total,
    0,
  );
  const weekdays = revision.weekdays;
  return {
    id: habit.id,
    name: revision.name,
    description: revision.description,
    status: getHabitStatus(habit, date) ?? "active",
    weekdays,
    frequency: formatWeekdays(weekdays),
    scheduledToday: isHabitScheduledOn(habit, date) && weekdayForDate(date) >= 0,
    dayStatus: knownStatus ?? currentEvent?.status ?? "pending",
    context: knownContext ?? currentEvent?.context,
    mainGoal: revision.mainGoal,
    minGoal: revision.minGoal,
    achievedLevel: currentEvent?.level,
    completionCount: completed,
    justificationCount: justified,
  };
}

export function getMonthDateRange(year: number, monthIndex: number): {
  start: LocalDate;
  end: LocalDate;
} {
  const start = `${year}-${String(monthIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-01`;
  const end = addLocalDays(start, new Date(year, monthIndex + 1, 0).getDate() - 1);
  return { start, end };
}

import type { ReactNode } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation } from 'wouter';
import { Award, CalendarDays, CheckSquare, CircleDollarSign, Compass, Leaf, Settings2 } from 'lucide-react';

const navigation = [
  { href: '/', label: 'Hoje', icon: Compass },
  { href: '/habits', label: 'Hábitos', icon: CheckSquare },
  { href: '/history', label: 'Histórico', icon: CalendarDays },
  { href: '/achievements', label: 'Conquistas', icon: Award },
  { href: '/goals', label: 'Metas', icon: CircleDollarSign },
  { href: '/settings', label: 'Configurações', icon: Settings2 },
];

function NavItems() {
  const [location] = useLocation();
  return <>{navigation.map(({ href, label, icon: Icon }) => (
    <Link href={href} key={href} className={`nav-link ${location === href ? 'active' : ''}`} data-testid={`link-nav-${label.toLowerCase().replaceAll(' ', '-')}`}>
      <Icon aria-hidden="true" /><span>{label}</span>
    </Link>
  ))}</>;
}

export function AppShell({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [location] = useLocation();
  const title = navigation.find((item) => item.href === location)?.label ?? 'Hoje';
  const today = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('pt-BR', { weekday: 'short', day: 'numeric', month: 'short' }).format(new Date());
  return <div className="app-shell">
    <aside className="sidebar">
      <Link href="/" className="brand" data-testid="link-brand">
        <span className="brand-mark"><Leaf size={19} strokeWidth={2.2} /></span>
        <span><span className="brand-name">Passo a Passo</span><span className="brand-sub">um dia de cada vez</span></span>
      </Link>
      <div className="nav-caption">Seu espaço</div>
      <nav className="nav-list" aria-label="Navegação principal"><NavItems /></nav>
      <div className="side-footer">
        <div className="offline-chip"><span className="offline-dot" />Seus dados ficam neste dispositivo</div>
        <p className="side-note">Cada pequeno passo conta.</p>
      </div>
    </aside>
    <main className="main-area">
      <header className="topbar">
        <span className="crumb">Seu espaço <span aria-hidden="true">/</span> <strong>{title}</strong></span>
        <div className="top-actions"><span className="date-pill">{today}</span><span className="avatar" aria-label="Passo a Passo">PP</span></div>
      </header>
      {children}
    </main>
    <nav className="mobile-nav" aria-label="Navegação principal"><NavItems /></nav>
  </div>;
}

ARQUIVO 14/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/components/error-boundary.tsx
================================================================
import {
  Component,
  type ComponentType,
  type ErrorInfo,
  type ReactNode,
} from 'react';

export interface ErrorFallbackProps {
  error: Error;
  resetError: () => void;
}

interface ErrorBoundaryProps {
  children: ReactNode;
  FallbackComponent?: ComponentType<ErrorFallbackProps>;
  /** Changing this clears a caught error. Pass the route to recover on navigation. */
  resetKey?: unknown;
}

interface ErrorBoundaryState {
  error: Error | null;
}

function toError(value: unknown): Error {
  if (value instanceof Error) {
    return value;
  }
  if (typeof value === 'string') {
    return new Error(value);
  }
  try {
    return new Error(JSON.stringify(value));
  } catch {
    return new Error(String(value));
  }
}

function DefaultFallback({ error, resetError }: ErrorFallbackProps) {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen w-full flex items-center justify-center bg-gray-50 p-6">
      <div className="max-w-lg w-full text-center">
        <h1 className="text-xl font-semibold text-gray-900">
          Something went wrong
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-600">
          This part of the app hit an error. The rest of the app is still
          running.
        </p>
        {/* Dev only: messages can carry API responses and other internals. */}
        {import.meta.env.DEV ? (
          <pre className="mt-4 overflow-x-auto rounded bg-gray-100 p-3 text-left text-xs text-gray-800">
            {error.message || String(error)}
          </pre>
        ) : null}
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={resetError}
          className="mt-4 rounded bg-gray-900 px-4 py-2 text-sm text-white hover:bg-gray-700"
        >
          Try again
        </button>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<
  ErrorBoundaryProps,
  ErrorBoundaryState
> {
  state: ErrorBoundaryState = { error: null };

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: unknown): ErrorBoundaryState {
    return { error: toError(error) };
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: unknown, info: ErrorInfo): void {
    console.error(
      'ErrorBoundary caught an error:',
      toError(error),
      info.componentStack,
    );
  }

  componentDidUpdate(prevProps: ErrorBoundaryProps): void {
    if (
      this.state.error !== null &&
      prevProps.resetKey !== this.props.resetKey
    ) {
      this.resetError();
    }
  }

  resetError = (): void => {
    this.setState({ error: null });
  };

  render(): ReactNode {
    const { error } = this.state;
    if (error === null) {
      return this.props.children;
    }
    const Fallback = this.props.FallbackComponent ?? DefaultFallback;
    return <Fallback error={error} resetError={this.resetError} />;
  }
}

import * as React from 'react';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { cva, type VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority';

const badgeVariants = cva(
  // @replit
  // Whitespace-nowrap: Badges should never wrap.
  'whitespace-nowrap inline-flex items-center rounded-md border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring focus:ring-offset-2' +
    ' hover-elevate ',
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          // @replit shadow-xs instead of shadow, no hover because we use hover-elevate
          'border-transparent bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-xs',
        secondary:
          // @replit no hover because we use hover-elevate
          'border-transparent bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground',
        destructive:
          // @replit shadow-xs instead of shadow, no hover because we use hover-elevate
          'border-transparent bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-xs',
        // @replit shadow-xs" - use badge outline variable
        outline: 'text-foreground border [border-color:var(--badge-outline)]',
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: 'default',
    },
  },
);

export interface BadgeProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants> {}

function Badge({ className, variant, ...props }: BadgeProps) {
  return (
    <div className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)} {...props} />
  );
}

export { Badge, badgeVariants };

ARQUIVO 22/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/components/ui/button-group.tsx

import { Slot } from '@radix-ui/react-slot';
import { Separator } from '@/components/ui/separator';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { cva, type VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority';

const buttonGroupVariants = cva(
  "flex w-fit items-stretch has-[>[data-slot=button-group]]:gap-2 [&>*]:focus-visible:relative [&>*]:focus-visible:z-10 has-[select[aria-hidden=true]:last-child]:[&>[data-slot=select-trigger]:last-of-type]:rounded-r-md [&>[data-slot=select-trigger]:not([class*='w-'])]:w-fit [&>input]:flex-1",
  {
    variants: {
      orientation: {
        horizontal:
          '[&>*:not(:first-child)]:rounded-l-none [&>*:not(:first-child)]:border-l-0 [&>*:not(:last-child)]:rounded-r-none',
        vertical:
          'flex-col [&>*:not(:first-child)]:rounded-t-none [&>*:not(:first-child)]:border-t-0 [&>*:not(:last-child)]:rounded-b-none',
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      orientation: 'horizontal',
    },
  },
);

function ButtonGroup({
  className,
  orientation,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'div'> & VariantProps<typeof buttonGroupVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      data-slot="button-group"
      data-orientation={orientation}
      className={cn(buttonGroupVariants({ orientation }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

function ButtonGroupText({
  className,
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<'div'> & {
  asChild?: boolean;
}) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : 'div';

  return (
    <Comp
      className={cn(
        "bg-muted shadow-xs flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border px-4 text-sm font-medium [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none",
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

function ButtonGroupSeparator({
  className,
  orientation = 'vertical',
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof Separator>) {
  return (
    <Separator
      data-slot="button-group-separator"
      orientation={orientation}
      className={cn(
        'bg-input relative !m-0 self-stretch data-[orientation=vertical]:h-auto',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export {
  ButtonGroup,
  ButtonGroupSeparator,
  ButtonGroupText,
  buttonGroupVariants,
};

ARQUIVO 23/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/components/ui/button.tsx

import * as React from 'react';
import { Slot } from '@radix-ui/react-slot';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import { cva, type VariantProps } from 'class-variance-authority';

const buttonVariants = cva(
  'inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0' +
    ' hover-elevate active-elevate-2',
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          // @replit: no hover, and add primary border
          'bg-primary text-primary-foreground border border-primary-border',
        destructive:
          'bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-sm border-destructive-border',
        outline:
          // @replit Shows the background color of whatever card / sidebar / accent background it is inside of.
          // Inherits the current text color. Uses shadow-xs. no shadow on active
          // No hover state
          ' border [border-color:var(--button-outline)] shadow-xs active:shadow-none ',
        secondary:
          // @replit border, no hover, no shadow, secondary border.
          'border bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground border border-secondary-border ',
        // @replit no hover, transparent border
        ghost: 'border border-transparent',
        link: 'text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline',
      },
      size: {
        // @replit changed sizes
        default: 'min-h-9 px-4 py-2',
        sm: 'min-h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs',
        lg: 'min-h-10 rounded-md px-8',
        icon: 'h-9 w-9',
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: 'default',
      size: 'default',
    },
  },
);

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
  asChild?: boolean;
}

const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : 'button';
    return (
      <Comp
        className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
        ref={ref}
        {...props}
      />
    );
  },
);
Button.displayName = 'Button';

export { Button, buttonVariants };

          defaultClassNames.day,

        },
        Chevron: ({ className, orientation, ...props }) => {
          if (orientation === 'left') {

              <ChevronLeftIcon className={cn('size-4', className)} {...props} />

          if (orientation === 'right') {
            return (
              <ChevronRightIcon

              <div className="flex size-[--cell-size] items-center justify-center text-center">

              </div>
            </td>

        ...components,
      }}
      {...props}
    />
  );

function CalendarDayButton({
  className,
  day,
  modifiers,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DayButton>) {
  const defaultClassNames = getDefaultClassNames();

  const ref = React.useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (modifiers.focused) ref.current?.focus();
  }, [modifiers.focused]);

  return (
    <Button
      ref={ref}
      variant="ghost"
      size="icon"
      data-day={day.date.toLocaleDateString()}
      data-selected-single={
        modifiers.selected &&
        !modifiers.range_start &&
        !modifiers.range_end &&
        !modifiers.range_middle
      }
      data-range-start={modifiers.range_start}
      data-range-end={modifiers.range_end}
      data-range-middle={modifiers.range_middle}
      className={cn(
        'data-[selected-single=true]:bg-primary data-[selected-single=true]:text-primary-foreground data-[range-middle=true]:bg-accent data-[range-middle=true]:text-accent-foreground data-[range-start=true]:bg-primary data-[range-start=true]:text-primary-foreground data-[range-end=true]:bg-primary data-[range-end=true]:text-primary-foreground group-data-[focused=true]/day:border-ring group-data-[focused=true]/day:ring-ring/50 flex aspect-square h-auto w-full min-w-[--cell-size] flex-col gap-1 font-normal leading-none data-[range-end=true]:rounded-md data-[range-middle=true]:rounded-none data-[range-start=true]:rounded-md group-data-[focused=true]/day:relative group-data-[focused=true]/day:z-10 group-data-[focused=true]/day:ring-[3px] [&>span]:text-xs [&>span]:opacity-70',
        defaultClassNames.day,
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}

export { Calendar, CalendarDayButton };

ARQUIVO 26/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/components/ui/carousel.tsx
================================================================
import * as React from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button';
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';
import useEmblaCarousel, {
  type UseEmblaCarouselType,
} from 'embla-carousel-react';
import { ArrowLeft, ArrowRight } from 'lucide-react';

type CarouselApi = UseEmblaCarouselType[1];
type UseCarouselParameters = Parameters<typeof useEmblaCarousel>;
type CarouselOptions = UseCarouselParameters[0];
type CarouselPlugin = UseCarouselParameters[1];

type CarouselProps = {
  opts?: CarouselOptions;
  plugins?: CarouselPlugin;
  orientation?: 'horizontal' | 'vertical';
  setApi?: (api: CarouselApi) => void;
};

type CarouselContextProps = {
  carouselRef: ReturnType<typeof useEmblaCarousel>[0];
  api: ReturnType<typeof useEmblaCarousel>[1];
  scrollPrev: () => void;
  scrollNext: () => void;
  canScrollPrev: boolean;
  canScrollNext: boolean;
} & CarouselProps;

const CarouselContext = React.createContext<CarouselContextProps | null>(null);

function useCarousel() {
  const context = React.useContext(CarouselContext);

  if (!context) {
    throw new Error('useCarousel must be used within a <Carousel />');
  }

  return context;
}

const Carousel = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & CarouselProps
>(
  (
    {
      orientation = 'horizontal',
      opts,
      setApi,
      plugins,
      className,
      children,
      ...props
    },
    ref,
  ) => {
    const [carouselRef, api] = useEmblaCarousel(
      {
        ...opts,
        axis: orientation === 'horizontal' ? 'x' : 'y',
      },
      plugins,
    );
    const [canScrollPrev, setCanScrollPrev] = React.useState(false);
    const [canScrollNext, setCanScrollNext] = React.useState(false);

    const onSelect = React.useCallback((api: CarouselApi) => {
      if (!api) {
        return;
      }

      setCanScrollPrev(api.canScrollPrev());
      setCanScrollNext(api.canScrollNext());
    }, []);

    const scrollPrev = React.useCallback(() => {
      api?.scrollPrev();
    }, [api]);

    const scrollNext = React.useCallback(() => {
      api?.scrollNext();
    }, [api]);

    const handleKeyDown = React.useCallback(
      (event: React.KeyboardEvent<HTMLDivElement>) => {
        if (event.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
          event.preventDefault();
          scrollPrev();
        } else if (event.key === 'ArrowRight') {
          event.preventDefault();
          scrollNext();
        }
      },
      [scrollPrev, scrollNext],
    );

    React.useEffect(() => {
      if (!api || !setApi) {
        return;
      }

      setApi(api);
    }, [api, setApi]);

    React.useEffect(() => {
      if (!api) {
        return;
      }

      onSelect(api);
      api.on('reInit', onSelect);
      api.on('select', onSelect);

      return () => {
        api?.off('select', onSelect);
      };
    }, [api, onSelect]);

    return (
      <CarouselContext.Provider
        value={{
          carouselRef,
          api: api,
          opts,
          orientation:
            orientation || (opts?.axis === 'y' ? 'vertical' : 'horizontal'),
          scrollPrev,
          scrollNext,
          canScrollPrev,
          canScrollNext,
        }}
      >
        <div
          ref={ref}
          onKeyDownCapture={handleKeyDown}
          className={cn('relative', className)}
          role="region"
          aria-roledescription="carousel"
          {...props}
        >
          {children}
        </div>
      </CarouselContext.Provider>
    );
  },
);
Carousel.displayName = 'Carousel';

const CarouselContent = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { carouselRef, orientation } = useCarousel();

  return (
    <div ref={carouselRef} className="overflow-hidden">
      <div
        ref={ref}
        className={cn(
          'flex',
          orientation === 'horizontal' ? '-ml-4' : '-mt-4 flex-col',
          className,
        )}
        {...props}
      />
    </div>
  );
});
CarouselContent.displayName = 'CarouselContent';

const CarouselItem = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const { orientation } = useCarousel();

  return (
    <div
      ref={ref}
      role="group"
      aria-roledescription="slide"
      className={cn(
        'min-w-0 shrink-0 grow-0 basis-full',
        orientation === 'horizontal' ? 'pl-4' : 'pt-4',
        className,
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  );
});
CarouselItem.displayName = 'CarouselItem';

const CarouselPrevious = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLButtonElement,
  React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>
>(({ className, variant = 'outline', size = 'icon', ...props }, ref) => {
  const { orientation, scrollPrev, canScrollPrev } = useCarousel();

  return (
    <Button
      ref={ref}
      variant={variant}
      size={size}
      className={cn(
        'absolute  h-8 w-8 rounded-full',
        orientation === 'horizontal'
          ? '-left-12 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2'
          : '-top-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-90',
        className,
      )}
      disabled={!canScrollPrev}
      onClick={scrollPrev}
      {...props}
    >
      <ArrowLeft className="h-4 w-4" />
      <span className="sr-only">Previous slide</span>
    </Button>
  );
});
CarouselPrevious.displayName = 'CarouselPrevious';

const CarouselNext = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLButtonElement,
  React.ComponentProps<typeof Button>
>(({ className, variant = 'outline', size = 'icon', ...props }, ref) => {
  const { orientation, scrollNext, canScrollNext } = useCarousel();

  return (
    <Button
      ref={ref}
      variant={variant}
      size={size}
      className={cn(
        'absolute h-8 w-8 rounded-full',
        orientation === 'horizontal'
          ? '-right-12 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2'
          : '-bottom-12 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 rotate-90',
        className,
      )}
      disabled={!canScrollNext}
      onClick={scrollNext}
      {...props}
    >
      <ArrowRight className="h-4 w-4" />
      <span className="sr-only">Next slide</span>
    </Button>
  );
});
CarouselNext.displayName = 'CarouselNext';

export {
  type CarouselApi,
  Carousel,
  CarouselContent,
  CarouselItem,
  CarouselPrevious,
  CarouselNext,
};

ARQUIVO 27/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/components/ui/chart.tsx
