import type {
  AppState,
  FinancialGoal,
  GoalContribution,
  GoalSummary,
  LocalDate,
} from "./model.ts";
import type { IdFactory } from "./habits.ts";

export interface GoalInput {
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  targetCents: number;
}

export interface ContributionInput {
  goalId: string;
  amountCents: number;
  date: LocalDate;
  note?: string;
}

export function createGoal(
  state: AppState,
  input: GoalInput,
  date: LocalDate,
  idFactory: IdFactory,
): AppState {
  validateGoalInput(input);
  const goal: FinancialGoal = {
    id: idFactory(),
    name: input.name.trim(),
    description: input.description?.trim() ?? "",
    targetCents: input.targetCents,
    createdOn: date,
  };
  return { ...state, goals: [...state.goals, goal] };
}

export function updateGoal(
  state: AppState,
  goalId: string,
  input: GoalInput,
): AppState {
  validateGoalInput(input);
  requireGoal(state, goalId);
  return {
    ...state,
    goals: state.goals.map((goal) =>
      goal.id === goalId
        ? {
            ...goal,
            name: input.name.trim(),
            description: input.description?.trim() ?? "",
            targetCents: input.targetCents,
          }
        : goal,
    ),
  };
}

export function archiveGoal(
  state: AppState,
  goalId: string,
  date: LocalDate,
): AppState {
  requireGoal(state, goalId);
  return {
    ...state,
    goals: state.goals.map((goal) =>
      goal.id === goalId ? { ...goal, archivedOn: date } : goal,
    ),
  };
}

export function addContribution(
  state: AppState,
  input: ContributionInput,
  now: Date,
  idFactory: IdFactory,
): AppState {
  const goal = requireGoal(state, input.goalId);
  validateAmount(input.amountCents);
  if (goal.archivedOn) {
    throw new Error("Não é possível adicionar valores a uma meta arquivada.");
  }
  if (input.date < goal.createdOn) {
    throw new Error("A data do aporte não pode ser anterior à criação da meta.");
  }
  const contribution: GoalContribution = {
    id: idFactory(),
    goalId: input.goalId,
    amountCents: input.amountCents,
    date: input.date,
    note: input.note?.trim() ?? "",
    createdAt: now.toISOString(),
    revisions: [],
  };
  return {
    ...state,
    contributions: [...state.contributions, contribution],
  };
}

export function correctContribution(
  state: AppState,
  contributionId: string,
  amountCents: number,
  note: string,
  now: Date,
): AppState {
  validateAmount(amountCents);
  const contribution = state.contributions.find(
    (item) => item.id === contributionId,
  );
  if (!contribution) throw new Error("Aporte não encontrado.");
  if (contribution.amountCents === amountCents && !note.trim()) return state;
  return {
    ...state,
    contributions: state.contributions.map((item) =>
      item.id === contributionId
        ? {
            ...item,
            amountCents,
            revisions: [
              ...item.revisions,
              {
                previousAmountCents: item.amountCents,
                correctedAt: now.toISOString(),
                note: note.trim(),
              },
            ],
          }
        : item,
    ),
  };
}

export function summarizeGoal(
  state: AppState,
  goalId: string,
): GoalSummary {
  const goal = requireGoal(state, goalId);
  const contributions = state.contributions
    .filter((item) => item.goalId === goalId)
    .sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date) || a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt));
  const contributedCents = contributions.reduce(
    (total, item) => addSafeCents(total, item.amountCents),
    0,
  );
  const remainingCents = Math.max(0, goal.targetCents - contributedCents);
  const percentComplete = Math.min(
    100,
    Math.round((contributedCents / goal.targetCents) * 1000) / 10,
  );
  return {
    goal,
    contributedCents,
    remainingCents,
    percentComplete,
    isComplete: contributedCents >= goal.targetCents,
    contributions,
  };
}

export function listGoalSummaries(state: AppState): GoalSummary[] {
  return state.goals
    .filter((goal) => !goal.archivedOn)
    .map((goal) => summarizeGoal(state, goal.id));
}

export function formatCents(amountCents: number, currency = "BRL"): string {
  validateAmount(amountCents, true);
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", {
    style: "currency",
    currency,
  }).format(amountCents / 100);
}

export function validateGoalInput(input: GoalInput): void {
  if (!input.name.trim()) throw new Error("Informe um nome para a meta.");
  if (input.name.trim().length > 100) {
    throw new Error("O nome da meta deve ter até 100 caracteres.");
  }
  if ((input.description?.length ?? 0) > 500) {
    throw new Error("A descrição deve ter até 500 caracteres.");
  }
  validateAmount(input.targetCents);
}

export function validateAmount(amountCents: number, allowZero = false): void {
  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(amountCents) ||
    (allowZero ? amountCents < 0 : amountCents <= 0)
  ) {
    throw new Error("O valor precisa ser informado em centavos, sem exceder o limite seguro.");
  }
}

function requireGoal(state: AppState, goalId: string): FinancialGoal {
  const goal = state.goals.find((item) => item.id === goalId);
  if (!goal) throw new Error("Meta financeira não encontrada.");
  return goal;
}

function addSafeCents(total: number, amount: number): number {
  const sum = total + amount;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(sum)) {
    throw new Error("A soma dos valores excede o limite seguro.");
  }
  return sum;
}

ARQUIVO 73/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/domain/habits.ts
================================================================
import { addLocalDays, compareLocalDates, toLocalDateFromTimestamp, toLocalDateKey, weekdayForDate } from "./dates.ts";
import type {
  AppState,
  DailySummary,
  GoalSpec,
  Habit,
  HabitDayEntry,
  HabitLifecycleStatus,
  HabitRevision,
  HabitStatus,
  LocalDate,
  ScheduledHabit,
} from "./model.ts";

export interface HabitInput {
  name: string;
  description?: string;
  weekdays: number[];
  mainGoal?: GoalSpec;
  minGoal?: GoalSpec;
}

export type IdFactory = () => string;

export function createHabit(
  state: AppState,
  input: HabitInput,
  date: LocalDate,
  idFactory: IdFactory,
): AppState {
  validateHabitInput(input);
  const habitId = idFactory();
  const revision: HabitRevision = {
    id: idFactory(),
    name: input.name.trim(),
    description: input.description?.trim() ?? "",
    weekdays: normalizeWeekdays(input.weekdays),
    mainGoal: input.mainGoal,
    minGoal: input.minGoal,
    effectiveFrom: date,
  };
  const habit: Habit = {
    id: habitId,
    createdOn: date,
    revisions: [revision],
    lifecycle: [{ id: idFactory(), status: "active", effectiveFrom: date }],
  };
  return { ...state, habits: [...state.habits, habit] };
}

export function updateHabit(
  state: AppState,
  habitId: string,
  input: HabitInput,
  effectiveFrom: LocalDate,
  idFactory: IdFactory,
): AppState {
  validateHabitInput(input);
  const habit = requireHabit(state, habitId);
  if (statusOnDate(habit, effectiveFrom) === "archived") {
    throw new Error("Um hábito excluído não pode ser alterado.");
  }
  const current = getHabitRevision(habit, effectiveFrom);
  if (!current) {
    throw new Error("Não foi possível encontrar a versão atual do hábito.");
  }
  const next = {
    name: input.name.trim(),
    description: input.description?.trim() ?? "",
    weekdays: normalizeWeekdays(input.weekdays),
    mainGoal: input.mainGoal,
    minGoal: input.minGoal,
  };
  if (
    current.name === next.name &&
    current.description === next.description &&
    sameNumbers(current.weekdays, next.weekdays) &&
    sameGoals(current.mainGoal, next.mainGoal) &&
    sameGoals(current.minGoal, next.minGoal)
  ) {
    return state;
  }
  const revision: HabitRevision = {
    id: idFactory(),
    ...next,
    effectiveFrom,
  };
  return {
    ...state,
    habits: state.habits.map((item) =>
      item.id === habitId
        ? { ...item, revisions: [...item.revisions, revision] }
        : item,
    ),
  };
}

export function setHabitLifecycle(
  state: AppState,
  habitId: string,
  status: HabitLifecycleStatus,
  effectiveFrom: LocalDate,
  idFactory: IdFactory,
): AppState {
  const habit = requireHabit(state, habitId);
  const current = statusOnDate(habit, effectiveFrom);
  if (current === "archived") {
    throw new Error("Um hábito excluído não pode ser retomado.");
  }
  if (current === status) return state;
  const event = { id: idFactory(), status, effectiveFrom };
  return {
    ...state,
    habits: state.habits.map((item) =>
      item.id === habitId
        ? { ...item, lifecycle: [...item.lifecycle, event] }
        : item,
    ),
  };
}

export function getHabitRevision(
  habit: Habit,
  date: LocalDate,
): HabitRevision | undefined {
  return latestEffective(habit.revisions, date);
}

export function getHabitStatus(
  habit: Habit,
  date: LocalDate,
): HabitLifecycleStatus | undefined {
  return latestEffective(habit.lifecycle, date)?.status;
}

export function isHabitScheduledOn(habit: Habit, date: LocalDate): boolean {
  if (compareLocalDates(habit.createdOn, date) > 0) return false;
  if (getHabitStatus(habit, date) !== "active") return false;
  const revision = getHabitRevision(habit, date);
  return revision?.weekdays.includes(weekdayForDate(date)) ?? false;
}

export function isHabitEntryScheduled(
  habit: Habit,
  entry: HabitDayEntry,
): boolean {
  if (
    compareLocalDates(habit.createdOn, entry.date) > 0 ||
    getHabitStatus(habit, entry.date) !== "active"
  ) {
    return false;
  }
  const revision = habit.revisions.find(
    (item) => item.id === entry.habitRevisionId,
  );
  return revision?.weekdays.includes(weekdayForDate(entry.date)) ?? false;
}

export function getDailySummary(state: AppState, date: LocalDate): DailySummary {
  const scheduled: ScheduledHabit[] = state.habits.flatMap((habit) => {
    if (
      compareLocalDates(habit.createdOn, date) > 0 ||
      getHabitStatus(habit, date) !== "active"
    ) return [];
    const entry = getHabitDayEntry(state, habit.id, date);
    const revision = entry
      ? habit.revisions.find((item) => item.id === entry.habitRevisionId)
      : getHabitRevision(habit, date);
    if (!revision) return [];
    if (!revision.weekdays.includes(weekdayForDate(date))) return [];
    const lastEvent = entry?.events.at(-1);
    return [{
      habit,
      revision,
      status: lastEvent?.status ?? "pending",
      context: lastEvent?.context,
    }];
  });
  const completed = scheduled.filter((item) => item.status === "completed").length;
  const justified = scheduled.filter((item) => item.status === "justified").length;
  const pending = scheduled.filter((item) => item.status === "pending").length;
  const neutral = scheduled.filter((item) => item.status === "neutral").length;
  return {
    date,
    scheduled,
    completed,
    justified,
    pending,
    neutral,
    isComplete: scheduled.length > 0 && completed === scheduled.length,
  };
}

export function getHabitDayEntry(
  state: AppState,
  habitId: string,
  date: LocalDate,
): HabitDayEntry | undefined {
  return state.habitDays.find(
    (item) => item.habitId === habitId && item.date === date,
  );
}

export function recordHabitStatus(
  state: AppState,
  habitId: string,
  date: LocalDate,
  status: HabitStatus,
  context: string | undefined,
  now: Date,
  idFactory: IdFactory,
  level?: "main" | "min",
): AppState {
  const today = toLocalDateKey(now);
  if (compareLocalDates(date, today) > 0) {
    throw new Error("Não é possível registrar um hábito em uma data futura.");
  }
  const habit = requireHabit(state, habitId);
  if (!isHabitScheduledOn(habit, date)) {
    throw new Error("Este hábito não estava programado para essa data.");
  }
  const previousEntry = getHabitDayEntry(state, habitId, date);
  const previousEvent = previousEntry?.events.at(-1);
  if (previousEvent?.status === status) return state;
  const revision = previousEntry
    ? habit.revisions.find((item) => item.id === previousEntry.habitRevisionId)
    : getHabitRevision(habit, date);
  if (
    getHabitStatus(habit, date) !== "active" ||
    !revision?.weekdays.includes(weekdayForDate(date))
  ) {
    throw new Error("Este hábito não estava programado para essa data.");
  }

  const cleanContext = context?.trim();
  if (status === "justified") {
    if (!cleanContext) {
      throw new Error("Informe o contexto da justificativa.");
    }
    const priorJustification = previousEntry?.events.find(
      (event) => event.status === "justified",
    );
    if (priorJustification) {
      throw new Error("Este registro já teve uma justificativa.");
    }
    if (countRecentJustifications(state, now) >= 2) {
      throw new Error("O limite de duas justificativas nos últimos sete dias foi atingido.");
    }
  }

  const event = {
    id: idFactory(),
    status,
    ...(status === "justified" ? { context: cleanContext } : {}),
    ...(status === "completed" && level ? { level } : {}),
    recordedOn: today,
    recordedAt: now.toISOString(),
  };
  const nextEntry: HabitDayEntry = {
    id: previousEntry?.id ?? `${habitId}:${date}`,
    habitId,
    date,
    habitRevisionId: previousEntry?.habitRevisionId ?? revision.id,
    events: [...(previousEntry?.events ?? []), event],
  };
  return {
    ...state,
    habitDays: previousEntry
      ? state.habitDays.map((item) => item.id === previousEntry.id ? nextEntry : item)
      : [...state.habitDays, nextEntry],
  };
}

export function countRecentJustifications(state: AppState, now: Date): number {
  const today = toLocalDateKey(now);
  const firstIncludedDate = addLocalDays(today, -6);
  return state.habitDays.reduce((total, entry) => {
    const count = entry.events.filter((event) => {
      if (event.status !== "justified") return false;
      const recordedOn = event.recordedOn || toLocalDateFromTimestamp(event.recordedAt);
      return recordedOn >= firstIncludedDate && recordedOn <= today;
    }).length;
    return total + count;
  }, 0);
}

export function validateHabitInput(input: HabitInput): void {
  if (!input.name.trim()) {
    throw new Error("Informe um nome para o hábito.");
  }
  if (input.name.trim().length > 80) {
    throw new Error("O nome do hábito deve ter até 80 caracteres.");
  }
  if ((input.description?.length ?? 0) > 500) {
    throw new Error("A descrição deve ter até 500 caracteres.");
  }
  if (
    input.weekdays.length < 1 ||
    input.weekdays.some((day) => !Number.isInteger(day) || day < 0 || day > 6)
  ) {
    throw new Error("Selecione ao menos um dia válido da semana.");
  }
  if (input.mainGoal && !isValidGoalSpec(input.mainGoal)) {
    throw new Error("A meta principal precisa ter um valor maior que zero e uma unidade.");
  }
  if (input.minGoal && !isValidGoalSpec(input.minGoal)) {
    throw new Error("A meta mínima precisa ter um valor maior que zero e uma unidade.");
  }
}

function isValidGoalSpec(goal: GoalSpec): boolean {
  return (
    Number.isFinite(goal.value) &&
    goal.value > 0 &&
    typeof goal.unit === "string" &&
    goal.unit.trim().length > 0
  );
}

function normalizeWeekdays(weekdays: number[]): number[] {
  return [...new Set(weekdays)].sort((a, b) => a - b);
}

function requireHabit(state: AppState, habitId: string): Habit {
  const habit = state.habits.find((item) => item.id === habitId);
  if (!habit) throw new Error("Hábito não encontrado.");
  return habit;
}

function latestEffective<T extends { effectiveFrom: LocalDate }>(
  items: T[],
  date: LocalDate,
): T | undefined {
  let latest: T | undefined;
  let latestIndex = -1;
  for (let index = 0; index < items.length; index += 1) {
    const item = items[index];
    if (
      compareLocalDates(item.effectiveFrom, date) <= 0 &&
      (!latest || compareLocalDates(item.effectiveFrom, latest.effectiveFrom) > 0 ||
        (item.effectiveFrom === latest.effectiveFrom && index > latestIndex))
    ) {
      latest = item;
      latestIndex = index;
    }
  }
  return latest;
}

function statusOnDate(
  habit: Habit,
  date: LocalDate,
): HabitLifecycleStatus | undefined {
  return getHabitStatus(habit, date);
}

function sameNumbers(a: number[], b: number[]): boolean {
  return a.length === b.length && a.every((value, index) => value === b[index]);
}

function sameGoals(a: GoalSpec | undefined, b: GoalSpec | undefined): boolean {
  if (a === b) return true;
  if (!a || !b) return false;
  return a.value === b.value && a.unit === b.unit;
}

export const APP_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1 as const;

export type LocalDate = string;
export type ThemeMode = "light" | "dark";
export type HabitStatus = "pending" | "completed" | "justified" | "neutral";
export type HabitLifecycleStatus = "active" | "paused" | "archived";

export interface AppPreferences {
  theme: ThemeMode;
  reducedMotion: boolean;
  weekStartsOn: 0 | 1;
  currency: string;
}

export interface GoalSpec {
  value: number;
  unit: string;
}

export interface HabitRevision {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  weekdays: number[];
  mainGoal?: GoalSpec;
  minGoal?: GoalSpec;
  effectiveFrom: LocalDate;
}

export interface HabitLifecycleEvent {
  id: string;
  status: HabitLifecycleStatus;
  effectiveFrom: LocalDate;
}

export interface Habit {
  id: string;
  createdOn: LocalDate;
  revisions: HabitRevision[];
  lifecycle: HabitLifecycleEvent[];
}

export interface HabitStatusEvent {
  id: string;
  status: HabitStatus;
  context?: string;
  level?: "main" | "min";
  recordedOn: LocalDate;
  recordedAt: string;
}

export interface HabitDayEntry {
  id: string;
  habitId: string;
  date: LocalDate;
  habitRevisionId: string;
  events: HabitStatusEvent[];
}

export interface ContributionRevision {
  previousAmountCents: number;
  correctedAt: string;
  note: string;
}

export interface GoalContribution {
  id: string;
  goalId: string;
  amountCents: number;
  date: LocalDate;
  note: string;
  createdAt: string;
  revisions: ContributionRevision[];
}

export interface FinancialGoal {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  targetCents: number;
  createdOn: LocalDate;
  archivedOn?: LocalDate;
}

export type AchievementCondition =
  | { kind: "completed-habits"; count: number }
  | { kind: "complete-days"; count: number }
  | { kind: "streak-days"; count: number }
  | { kind: "total-xp"; amountXp: number }
  | { kind: "saved-cents"; amountCents: number };

export interface AchievementDefinition {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  description: string;
  rewardXp: number;
  condition: AchievementCondition;
}

export interface AchievementUnlock {
  achievementId: string;
  unlockedOn: LocalDate;
  unlockedAt: string;
  rewardXp: number;
}

export interface AppState {
  schemaVersion: typeof APP_SCHEMA_VERSION;
  preferences: AppPreferences;
  habits: Habit[];
  habitDays: HabitDayEntry[];
  goals: FinancialGoal[];
  contributions: GoalContribution[];
  achievementUnlocks: AchievementUnlock[];
}

export interface ScheduledHabit {
  habit: Habit;
  revision: HabitRevision;
  status: HabitStatus;
  context?: string;
}

export interface DailySummary {
  date: LocalDate;
  scheduled: ScheduledHabit[];
  completed: number;
  justified: number;
  pending: number;
  neutral: number;
  isComplete: boolean;
}

export interface LevelThreshold {
  level: number;
  minimumXp: number;
  title?: string;
}

export interface ProgressionRules {
  habitCompletionXp: number;
  fullDayBonusXp: number;
  levels: LevelThreshold[];
  achievements: AchievementDefinition[];
}

export interface XpLedgerEntry {
  id: string;
  date: LocalDate;
  kind: "habit-completion" | "full-day-bonus" | "achievement";
  sourceId: string;
  amountXp: number;
}

export interface LevelProgress {
  level: number;
  title?: string;
  totalXp: number;
  xpIntoLevel: number;
  xpToNextLevel: number | null;
  nextLevel: number | null;
}

export interface GoalSummary {
  goal: FinancialGoal;
  contributedCents: number;
  remainingCents: number;
  percentComplete: number;
  isComplete: boolean;
  contributions: GoalContribution[];
}

ARQUIVO 76/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/domain/progression.ts

import { addLocalDays, compareLocalDates, toLocalDateKey } from "./dates.ts";
import {
  getDailySummary,
  isHabitEntryScheduled,
} from "./habits.ts";
import type {
  AchievementCondition,
  AchievementDefinition,
  AchievementUnlock,
  AppState,
  LevelProgress,
  LocalDate,
  ProgressionRules,
  XpLedgerEntry,
} from "./model.ts";
import { listGoalSummaries } from "./finance.ts";

export interface StreakSummary {
  current: number;
  best: number;
  completedDays: number;
  completedHabits: number;
}

export function getStreakSummary(
  state: AppState,
  today: LocalDate,
): StreakSummary {
  const firstDate = state.habits.reduce<LocalDate | undefined>(
    (earliest, habit) =>
      !earliest || compareLocalDates(habit.createdOn, earliest) < 0
        ? habit.createdOn
        : earliest,
    undefined,
  );
  if (!firstDate || compareLocalDates(firstDate, today) > 0) {
    return { current: 0, best: 0, completedDays: 0, completedHabits: 0 };
  }

  let current = 0;
  let best = 0;
  let completedDays = 0;
  let completedHabits = 0;
  let cursor = firstDate;
  while (compareLocalDates(cursor, today) <= 0) {
    const summary = getDailySummary(state, cursor);
    completedHabits += summary.completed;
    if (summary.isComplete) {
      current += 1;
      best = Math.max(best, current);
      completedDays += 1;
    } else if (summary.scheduled.length > 0) {
      current = 0;
    }
    cursor = addLocalDays(cursor, 1);
  }
  return { current, best, completedDays, completedHabits };
}

export function buildXpLedger(
  state: AppState,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
): XpLedgerEntry[] {
  validateProgressionRules(rules);
  const ledger: XpLedgerEntry[] = [];
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  for (const entry of state.habitDays) {
    const currentStatus = entry.events.at(-1)?.status;
    const habit = state.habits.find((item) => item.id === entry.habitId);
    if (
      currentStatus !== "completed" ||
      !habit ||
      !isHabitEntryScheduled(habit, entry)
    ) {
      continue;
    }
    const id = `habit-completion:${entry.habitId}:${entry.date}`;
    if (seen.has(id)) continue;
    seen.add(id);
    ledger.push({
      id,
      sourceId: `${entry.habitId}:${entry.date}`,
      date: entry.date,
      kind: "habit-completion",
      amountXp: rules.habitCompletionXp,
    });
  }

  const completionDates = new Set(
    state.habitDays
      .filter((entry) => entry.events.at(-1)?.status === "completed")
      .map((entry) => entry.date),
  );
  for (const date of completionDates) {
    const summary = getDailySummary(state, date);
    if (!summary.isComplete) continue;
    const id = `full-day-bonus:${date}`;
    if (seen.has(id)) continue;
    seen.add(id);
    ledger.push({
      id,
      sourceId: date,
      date,
      kind: "full-day-bonus",
      amountXp: rules.fullDayBonusXp,
    });
  }

  for (const unlock of state.achievementUnlocks) {
    const id = `achievement:${unlock.achievementId}`;
    if (seen.has(id)) continue;
    seen.add(id);
    ledger.push({
      id,
      sourceId: unlock.achievementId,
      date: unlock.unlockedOn,
      kind: "achievement",
      amountXp: unlock.rewardXp,
    });
  }
  return ledger.sort(
    (a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date) || a.id.localeCompare(b.id),
  );
}

export function getTotalXp(
  state: AppState,
  rules: ProgressionRules,
): number {
  return buildXpLedger(state, rules).reduce((total, entry) => {
    const sum = total + entry.amountXp;
    if (!Number.isSafeInteger(sum)) throw new Error("O total de XP excedeu o limite seguro.");
    return sum;
  }, 0);
}

export function getLevelProgress(
  totalXp: number,
  levels: ProgressionRules["levels"],
): LevelProgress {
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(totalXp) || totalXp < 0) {
    throw new Error("O total de XP é inválido.");
  }
  const ordered = [...levels].sort((a, b) => a.minimumXp - b.minimumXp);
  if (
    ordered.length === 0 ||
    ordered[0].level !== 1 ||
    ordered[0].minimumXp !== 0 ||
    ordered.some((item, index) =>
      item.level !== index + 1 ||
      !Number.isSafeInteger(item.minimumXp) ||
      item.minimumXp < 0 ||
      (index > 0 && item.minimumXp <= ordered[index - 1].minimumXp),
    )
  ) {
    throw new Error("A tabela de níveis está ausente ou inválida.");
  }
  let current = ordered[0];
  let next = ordered[1];
  for (let index = 1; index < ordered.length; index += 1) {
    if (totalXp < ordered[index].minimumXp) {
      next = ordered[index];
      break;
    }
    current = ordered[index];
    next = ordered[index + 1];
  }
  return {
    level: current.level,
    title: current.title,
    totalXp,
    xpIntoLevel: totalXp - current.minimumXp,
    xpToNextLevel: next ? Math.max(0, next.minimumXp - totalXp) : null,
    nextLevel: next?.level ?? null,
  };
}

export function unlockEligibleAchievements(
  state: AppState,
  catalog: AchievementDefinition[],
  rules: ProgressionRules,
  today = toLocalDateKey(),
  now = new Date(),
): AppState {
  let nextState = state;
  const progression = getStreakSummary(state, today);
  const contributedCents = listGoalSummaries(state).reduce(
    (total, summary) => total + summary.contributedCents,
    0,
  );
  while (true) {
    const unlocked = new Set(nextState.achievementUnlocks.map((item) => item.achievementId));
    const totalXp = buildXpLedger(nextState, rules).reduce(
      (total, item) => total + item.amountXp,
      0,
    );
    const newlyUnlocked: AchievementUnlock[] = catalog
      .filter((achievement) =>
        !unlocked.has(achievement.id) &&
        isAchievementEligible(achievement.condition, {
        completedHabits: progression.completedHabits,
        completeDays: progression.completedDays,
        streakDays: progression.best,
        savedCents: contributedCents,
        totalXp,
        }),
      )
      .map((achievement) => ({
        achievementId: achievement.id,
        unlockedOn: today,
        unlockedAt: now.toISOString(),
        rewardXp: achievement.rewardXp,
      }));
    if (newlyUnlocked.length === 0) return nextState;
    nextState = {
      ...nextState,
      achievementUnlocks: [...nextState.achievementUnlocks, ...newlyUnlocked],
    };
  }
}

export function isAchievementEligible(
  condition: AchievementCondition,
  metrics: {
    completedHabits: number;
    completeDays: number;
    streakDays: number;
    savedCents: number;
    totalXp: number;
  },
): boolean {
  switch (condition.kind) {
    case "completed-habits":
      return metrics.completedHabits >= condition.count;
    case "complete-days":
      return metrics.completeDays >= condition.count;
    case "streak-days":
      return metrics.streakDays >= condition.count;
    case "total-xp":
      return metrics.totalXp >= condition.amountXp;
    case "saved-cents":
      return metrics.savedCents >= condition.amountCents;
  }
}

export function validateProgressionRules(rules: ProgressionRules): void {
  if (
    !Number.isSafeInteger(rules.habitCompletionXp) ||
    rules.habitCompletionXp < 0 ||
    !Number.isSafeInteger(rules.fullDayBonusXp) ||
    rules.fullDayBonusXp < 0
  ) {
    throw new Error("As regras de XP estão ausentes ou inválidas.");
  }
  getLevelProgress(0, rules.levels);
  const ids = new Set<string>();
  for (const achievement of rules.achievements) {
    if (
      !achievement.id.trim() ||
      ids.has(achievement.id) ||
      !Number.isSafeInteger(achievement.rewardXp) ||
      achievement.rewardXp < 0 ||
      !isValidCondition(achievement.condition)
    ) {
      throw new Error("O catálogo de conquistas contém dados inválidos.");
    }
    ids.add(achievement.id);
  }
}

function isValidCondition(condition: AchievementCondition): boolean {
  switch (condition.kind) {
    case "completed-habits":
    case "complete-days":
    case "streak-days":
      return Number.isSafeInteger(condition.count) && condition.count > 0;
    case "total-xp":
      return Number.isSafeInteger(condition.amountXp) && condition.amountXp > 0;
    case "saved-cents":
      return Number.isSafeInteger(condition.amountCents) && condition.amountCents > 0;
  }
}

import { APP_SCHEMA_VERSION, type AppState } from "./model.ts";

export function createInitialState(): AppState {
  return {
    schemaVersion: APP_SCHEMA_VERSION,
    preferences: {
      theme: "light",
      reducedMotion: false,
      weekStartsOn: 1,
      currency: "BRL",
    },
    habits: [],
    habitDays: [],
    goals: [],
    contributions: [],
    achievementUnlocks: [],
  };
}

export function createId(): string {
  if (!globalThis.crypto?.randomUUID) {
    throw new Error("Este navegador não oferece geração segura de identificadores.");
  }
  return globalThis.crypto.randomUUID();
}

ARQUIVO 78/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/hooks/use-mobile.tsx

import * as React from 'react';

const MOBILE_BREAKPOINT = 768;

export function useIsMobile() {
  const [isMobile, setIsMobile] = React.useState<boolean | undefined>(
    undefined,
  );

  React.useEffect(() => {
    const mql = window.matchMedia(`(max-width: ${MOBILE_BREAKPOINT - 1}px)`);
    const onChange = () => {
      setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT);
    };
    mql.addEventListener('change', onChange);
    setIsMobile(window.innerWidth < MOBILE_BREAKPOINT);
    return () => mql.removeEventListener('change', onChange);
  }, []);

  return !!isMobile;
}

ARQUIVO 79/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/hooks/use-toast.ts
================================================================
import * as React from 'react';
import type { ToastActionElement, ToastProps } from '@/components/ui/toast';

const TOAST_LIMIT = 1;
const TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY = 1000000;

type ToasterToast = ToastProps & {
  id: string;
  title?: React.ReactNode;
  description?: React.ReactNode;
  action?: ToastActionElement;
};

const actionTypes = {
  ADD_TOAST: 'ADD_TOAST',
  UPDATE_TOAST: 'UPDATE_TOAST',
  DISMISS_TOAST: 'DISMISS_TOAST',
  REMOVE_TOAST: 'REMOVE_TOAST',
} as const;

let count = 0;

function genId() {
  count = (count + 1) % Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER;
  return count.toString();
}

type ActionType = typeof actionTypes;

type Action =
  | {
      type: ActionType['ADD_TOAST'];
      toast: ToasterToast;
    }
  | {
      type: ActionType['UPDATE_TOAST'];
      toast: Partial<ToasterToast>;
    }
  | {
      type: ActionType['DISMISS_TOAST'];
      toastId?: ToasterToast['id'];
    }
  | {
      type: ActionType['REMOVE_TOAST'];
      toastId?: ToasterToast['id'];
    };

interface State {
  toasts: ToasterToast[];
}

const toastTimeouts = new Map<string, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>();

const addToRemoveQueue = (toastId: string) => {
  if (toastTimeouts.has(toastId)) {
    return;
  }

  const timeout = setTimeout(() => {
    toastTimeouts.delete(toastId);
    dispatch({
      type: 'REMOVE_TOAST',
      toastId: toastId,
    });
  }, TOAST_REMOVE_DELAY);

  toastTimeouts.set(toastId, timeout);
};

export const reducer = (state: State, action: Action): State => {
  switch (action.type) {
    case 'ADD_TOAST':
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: [action.toast, ...state.toasts].slice(0, TOAST_LIMIT),
      };

    case 'UPDATE_TOAST':
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === action.toast.id ? { ...t, ...action.toast } : t,
        ),
      };

    case 'DISMISS_TOAST': {
      const { toastId } = action;

      // ! Side effects ! - This could be extracted into a dismissToast() action,
      // but I'll keep it here for simplicity
      if (toastId) {
        addToRemoveQueue(toastId);
      } else {
        state.toasts.forEach((toast) => {
          addToRemoveQueue(toast.id);
        });
      }

      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.map((t) =>
          t.id === toastId || toastId === undefined
            ? {
                ...t,
                open: false,
              }
            : t,
        ),
      };
    }
    case 'REMOVE_TOAST':
      if (action.toastId === undefined) {
        return {
          ...state,
          toasts: [],
        };
      }
      return {
        ...state,
        toasts: state.toasts.filter((t) => t.id !== action.toastId),
      };
  }
};

const listeners: Array<(state: State) => void> = [];

let memoryState: State = { toasts: [] };

function dispatch(action: Action) {
  memoryState = reducer(memoryState, action);
  listeners.forEach((listener) => {
    listener(memoryState);
  });
}

type Toast = Omit<ToasterToast, 'id'>;

function toast({ ...props }: Toast) {
  const id = genId();

  const update = (props: ToasterToast) =>
    dispatch({
      type: 'UPDATE_TOAST',
      toast: { ...props, id },
    });
  const dismiss = () => dispatch({ type: 'DISMISS_TOAST', toastId: id });

  dispatch({
    type: 'ADD_TOAST',
    toast: {
      ...props,
      id,
      open: true,
      onOpenChange: (open) => {
        if (!open) dismiss();
      },
    },
  });

  return {
    id: id,
    dismiss,
    update,
  };
}

function useToast() {
  const [state, setState] = React.useState<State>(memoryState);

  React.useEffect(() => {
    listeners.push(setState);
    return () => {
      const index = listeners.indexOf(setState);
      if (index > -1) {
        listeners.splice(index, 1);
      }
    };
  }, [state]);

  return {
    ...state,
    toast,
    dismiss: (toastId?: string) => dispatch({ type: 'DISMISS_TOAST', toastId }),
  };
}

export { useToast, toast };

ARQUIVO 82/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/pages/common.tsx

import type { ReactNode } from "react";

export function PageHeading({
  eyebrow,
  title,
  subtitle,
  action,
}: {
  eyebrow: string;
  title: string;
  subtitle: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="page-head">
      <div>
        <div className="eyebrow">{eyebrow}</div>
        <h1>{title}</h1>
        <p className="subhead">{subtitle}</p>
      </div>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

export function CardHeading({
  title,
  detail,
  action,
}: {
  title: string;
  detail?: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="card-title-row">
      <div>
        <h2 className="card-title">{title}</h2>
        {detail && <div className="card-kicker">{detail}</div>}
      </div>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

export function EmptyState({
  icon,
  title,
  body,
  action,
}: {
  icon: ReactNode;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  action?: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="empty-state">
      <div className="empty-icon">{icon}</div>
      <h3>{title}</h3>
      <p>{body}</p>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

export function StatusLabel({ children, tone = "neutral" }: { children: ReactNode; tone?: string }) {
  return <span className={`status-label status-${tone}`}>{children}</span>;
}

ARQUIVO 83/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/pages/dashboard-page.tsx

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import {
  ArrowRight,
  Award,
  Check,
  CircleDollarSign,
  Flame,
  Leaf,
  Plus,
  Sparkles,
  Star,
  Trophy,
} from "lucide-react";
import { getGreeting, formatLocalDate, formatMoneyForState, getWeeklyReport } from "@/app/view-models";
import type { HabitView } from "@/app/view-models";
import type { GoalSummary } from "@/domain/model";
import type {
  AchievementDefinition,
  AppState,
  DailySummary,
  LevelProgress,
} from "@/domain/model";
import type { StreakSummary } from "@/domain/progression";
import type { StateChangeResult } from "@/app/app-data-controller";
import { CardHeading, EmptyState, StatusLabel } from "./common";

export function DashboardPage({
  state,
  today,
  habits,
  summary,
  streaks,
  goals,
  message,
  level,
  xpTotal,
  achievements,
  onAddHabit,
  onSetStatus,
  onJustify,
}: {
  state: AppState;
  today: string;
  habits: HabitView[];
  summary: DailySummary;
  streaks: StreakSummary;
  goals: GoalSummary[];
  message: string;
  level: LevelProgress | null;
  xpTotal: number | null;
  achievements: AchievementDefinition[];
  onAddHabit: () => void;
  onSetStatus: (habitId: string, status: "pending" | "completed" | "neutral", level?: "main" | "min") => StateChangeResult;
  onJustify: (habit: HabitView) => void;
}) {
  const completion = summary.scheduled.length
    ? Math.round(summary.completed / summary.scheduled.length * 100)
    : 0;
  const lockedAchievements = achievements
    .filter((item) => !state.achievementUnlocks.some((unlock) => unlock.achievementId === item.id))
    .slice(0, 2);
  const unlockedCount = state.achievementUnlocks.length;
  const [celebratingHabitId, setCelebratingHabitId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const celebrationTimer = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const hasCompletedToday = summary.scheduled.length > 0 &&
    summary.completed === summary.scheduled.length;
  const week = getWeeklyReport(state, today);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    if (celebrationTimer.current !== null) {
      window.clearTimeout(celebrationTimer.current);
    }
  }, []);

  const toggleHabit = (habit: HabitView, level?: "main" | "min") => {
    const nextStatus = habit.dayStatus === "completed" ? "pending" : "completed";
    const result = onSetStatus(habit.id, nextStatus, level);
    if (!result.ok) return;
    if (nextStatus === "completed") {
      setCelebratingHabitId(habit.id);
      if (celebrationTimer.current !== null) {
        window.clearTimeout(celebrationTimer.current);
      }
      celebrationTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => {
        setCelebratingHabitId(null);
        celebrationTimer.current = null;
      }, 900);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="content fade-up">
      <section className="welcome-card">
        <div className="welcome-copy">
          <div className="eyebrow">{getGreeting()}</div>
          <h1>Um passo de cada vez.</h1>
          <p>{message}</p>
        </div>
        <div className="date-label">{formatLocalDate(today)}</div>
      </section>

      <div className="dashboard-grid">
        <div>
          <section className="journey-progress-card" aria-label="Resumo do seu progresso">
            <div className="journey-progress-main">
              <div className="journey-progress-copy">
                <span className="journey-overline">O caminho de hoje</span>
                <strong data-testid="stat-daily-progress">
                  {summary.scheduled.length ? `${summary.completed} de ${summary.scheduled.length}` : "Seu primeiro passo"}
                </strong>
                <span>{summary.scheduled.length ? "hábitos concluídos" : "começa quando você quiser"}</span>
              </div>
              <div
                className={`journey-progress-ring ${hasCompletedToday ? "is-complete" : ""}`}
                style={{ "--progress": `${completion}%` } as React.CSSProperties}
                role="img"
                aria-label={`${completion}% do plano diário concluído`}
              >
                <span>{summary.scheduled.length ? `${completion}%` : <Leaf aria-hidden="true" />}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="journey-progress-track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Progresso dos hábitos de hoje" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={completion}>
              <span style={{ width: `${completion}%` }} />
            </div>
            <div className="journey-stat-row">
              <div className="journey-stat" data-testid="stat-current-streak">
                <span className="journey-stat-icon streak"><Flame aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                <div><small>Sequência</small><strong>{streaks.current}</strong></div>
                <span className="journey-stat-caption">recorde de {streaks.best} dias</span>
              </div>
              <div className="journey-stat" data-testid="stat-xp">
                <span className="journey-stat-icon xp"><Sparkles aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                <div><small>Experiência</small><strong>{xpTotal === null ? "—" : `${xpTotal} XP`}</strong></div>
                <span className="journey-stat-caption">{xpTotal === null ? "regras pendentes" : "atividades válidas"}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="card">
            <CardHeading
              title="Hábitos de hoje"
              detail={`${summary.scheduled.length} ${summary.scheduled.length === 1 ? "hábito programado" : "hábitos programados"}`}
              action={
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="button button-soft"
                  onClick={onAddHabit}
                  data-testid="button-add-habit-dashboard"
                >
                  <Plus aria-hidden="true" />
                  Adicionar
                </button>
              }
            />
            {hasCompletedToday && (
              <div className="daily-complete-banner" role="status" data-testid="text-day-complete">
                <span className="daily-complete-icon"><Sparkles aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                <span><strong>Você completou o plano de hoje.</strong><small>Um passo consistente faz diferença.</small></span>
              </div>
            )}
            {habits.length ? (
              <div className="habit-list">
                {habits.map((habit) => (
                  <div
                    className={`habit-row ${habit.dayStatus === "completed" ? "is-complete" : ""} ${celebratingHabitId === habit.id ? "just-completed" : ""}`}
                    key={habit.id}
                    data-testid={`row-today-habit-${habit.id}`}
                  >
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      className={`check-button ${habit.dayStatus === "completed" ? "done" : ""}`}
                      aria-label={habit.dayStatus === "completed" ? `Desmarcar ${habit.name}` : `Concluir ${habit.name}`}
                      onClick={() => toggleHabit(habit, habit.mainGoal ? "main" : undefined)}
                      data-testid={`button-toggle-habit-${habit.id}`}
                    >
                      {habit.dayStatus === "completed" && <Check aria-hidden="true" />}
                    </button>
                    <div className="habit-main">
                      <div className="habit-name">{habit.name}</div>
                      <div className="habit-desc">
                        {habit.dayStatus === "justified" ? habit.context
                          : habit.mainGoal || habit.minGoal
                            ? [
                                habit.mainGoal ? `Principal: ${habit.mainGoal.value} ${habit.mainGoal.unit}` : "",
                                habit.minGoal ? `Mínima: ${habit.minGoal.value} ${habit.minGoal.unit}` : "",
                              ].filter(Boolean).join(" · ")
                            : habit.description || habit.frequency}
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <div className="habit-actions">
                      {celebratingHabitId === habit.id && (
                        <span className="completion-pop" aria-hidden="true">
                          <Star /> Boa!
                        </span>
                      )}
                      {habit.dayStatus === "justified" ? (
                        <StatusLabel tone="justified">Justificado</StatusLabel>
                      ) : habit.dayStatus === "neutral" ? (
                        <StatusLabel>Neutro</StatusLabel>
                      ) : (
                        <StatusLabel tone={habit.dayStatus === "completed" ? "complete" : "pending"}>
                          {habit.dayStatus === "completed"
                            ? habit.achievedLevel === "main"
                              ? "Meta principal"
                              : habit.achievedLevel === "min"
                                ? "Meta mínima"
                                : "Concluído"
                            : "Pendente"}
                        </StatusLabel>
                      )}
                      {habit.dayStatus !== "justified" && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="text-action"
                          onClick={() => onJustify(habit)}
                          data-testid={`button-justify-habit-${habit.id}`}
                        >
                          Justificar
                        </button>
                      )}
                      {habit.dayStatus === "pending" && habit.minGoal && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="text-action secondary-action"
                          onClick={() => toggleHabit(habit, "min")}
                          data-testid={`button-min-habit-${habit.id}`}
                        >
                          Só a mínima
                        </button>
                      )}
                      {habit.dayStatus === "pending" && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="text-action secondary-action"
                          onClick={() => onSetStatus(habit.id, "neutral")}
                          data-testid={`button-neutral-habit-${habit.id}`}
                        >
                          Neutro
                        </button>
                      )}
                      {habit.dayStatus === "neutral" && (
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          className="text-action secondary-action"
                          onClick={() => onSetStatus(habit.id, "pending")}
                          data-testid={`button-reactivate-habit-${habit.id}`}
                        >
                          Reativar
                        </button>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <EmptyState
                icon={<Leaf aria-hidden="true" />}
                title={summary.scheduled.length === 0 ? "Sem hábitos para hoje" : "Comece com um passo"}
                body={summary.scheduled.length === 0
                  ? "Hoje não há hábitos programados para sua rotina."
                  : "Adicione um hábito que faça sentido para você."}
                action={
                  summary.scheduled.length === 0 ? undefined : (
                    <button className="button button-primary" onClick={onAddHabit} data-testid="button-empty-add-habit">
                      <Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Criar primeiro hábito
                    </button>
                  )
                }
              />
            )}
          </section>
        </div>

        <div className="side-stack">
          <section className="level-panel" data-testid="panel-level">
            <div className="level-heading">
              <span className="level-title">Sua jornada</span>
              <span className="level-badge">Nível</span>
            </div>
            <div className="level-number">{level?.level ?? "—"}</div>
            <div className="level-note">
              {level
                ? level.title ?? "Seu progresso, no seu ritmo."
                : "A tabela de níveis original ainda não foi disponibilizada."}
            </div>
            {level && level.nextLevel !== null && (
              <>
                <div className="level-track" aria-label={`${level.xpIntoLevel} XP nesta faixa`}><span /></div>
                <div className="level-foot">
                  <span>{level.xpIntoLevel} XP nesta faixa</span>
                  <span>{level.xpToNextLevel} XP até o próximo nível</span>
                </div>
              </>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="card">
            <CardHeading
              title="Revisão da semana"
              detail={`${week.completedDays}/${week.scheduledDays} dias ativos`}
            />
            <div className="week-report" data-testid="panel-week-report">
              <div className="week-report-stats">
                <div><strong>{week.totalCompleted}</strong><span>conclusões</span></div>
                <div><strong>{week.mainGoals}</strong><span>metas principais</span></div>
                <div><strong>{week.minGoals}</strong><span>metas mínimas</span></div>
              </div>
              <p className="week-suggestion">{week.suggestion}</p>
            </div>
          </section>

          <section className="card">
            <CardHeading
              title="Próximas conquistas"
              detail={`${unlockedCount} desbloqueadas`}
              action={<Link href="/achievements" className="tiny-link">Ver todas <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" /></Link>}
            />
            {lockedAchievements.length ? (
              <div className="mini-achievements">
                {lockedAchievements.map((achievement) => (
                  <div className="mini-achievement" key={achievement.id}>
                    <span className="achievement-mark"><Award aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                    <div><strong>{achievement.name}</strong><span>{achievement.description}</span></div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mini-empty" data-testid="text-achievements-empty">
                <div className="achievement-mark" style={{ margin: "0 auto 12px" }}><Trophy aria-hidden="true" /></div>
                O catálogo original de conquistas ainda não foi carregado.
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <section className="card">
            <CardHeading
              title="Metas financeiras"
              action={<Link href="/goals" className="tiny-link">Abrir metas <ArrowRight aria-hidden="true" /></Link>}
            />
            {goals.length ? (
              <div className="goal-summary-list">
                {goals.map((summary) => (
                  <div className="goal-summary-row" key={summary.goal.id}>
                    <div className="goal-summary-top"><strong>{summary.goal.name}</strong><span>{summary.percentComplete}%</span></div>
                    <div className="progress-track"><div className="progress-fill progress-blue" style={{ width: `${summary.percentComplete}%` }} /></div>
                    <span className="goal-summary-amount">
                      {formatMoneyForState(state, summary.contributedCents)} de {formatMoneyForState(state, summary.goal.targetCents)}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mini-empty">
                <CircleDollarSign aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>Uma meta pode ajudar a acompanhar seus próximos passos.</span>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>

          <div className="daily-message" data-testid="text-contextual-message">
            <Sparkles aria-hidden="true" />
            <span>{message}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

ARQUIVO 84/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/pages/goals-achievements-settings-pages.tsx
================================================================
import { useState } from "react";
import {
  Archive,
  ArrowDownToLine,
  Award,
  Check,
  CircleDollarSign,
  Edit3,
  Leaf,
  Plus,
  ShieldCheck,
  Trash2,
  Trophy,
} from "lucide-react";
import { formatLocalDate, formatMoneyForState } from "@/app/view-models";
import type { AppPreferences, AchievementDefinition, AppState, GoalSummary } from "@/domain/model";
import { CardHeading, EmptyState, PageHeading, StatusLabel } from "./common";

export function AchievementsPage({
  state,
  catalog,
}: {
  state: AppState;
  catalog: AchievementDefinition[];
}) {
  const unlocked = new Map(state.achievementUnlocks.map((item) => [item.achievementId, item]));
  const entries = catalog.map((achievement) => ({
    achievement,
    unlock: unlocked.get(achievement.id),
  }));
  const unlockedCount = entries.filter((entry) => entry.unlock).length;

  return (
    <div className="content fade-up">
      <PageHeading
        eyebrow="Pequenas conquistas"
        title="Conquistas"
        subtitle="Marcos reconhecem o caminho que você percorre, no seu tempo."
      />
      <section className="card page-card">
        <CardHeading title="Seu catálogo" detail={`${unlockedCount} de ${entries.length} desbloqueadas`} />
        {entries.length ? (
          <div className="catalog-grid">
            {entries.map(({ achievement, unlock }) => (
              <article
                className={`achievement-card ${unlock ? "unlocked" : "locked"}`}
                key={achievement.id}
                data-testid={`card-achievement-${achievement.id}`}
              >
                <div className="achievement-top">
                  <span className="achievement-mark">{unlock ? <Award aria-hidden="true" /> : <Trophy aria-hidden="true" />}</span>
                  <StatusLabel tone={unlock ? "complete" : "pending"}>{unlock ? "Desbloqueada" : "Bloqueada"}</StatusLabel>
                </div>
                <h3>{achievement.name}</h3>
                <p>{achievement.description}</p>
                {unlock ? (
                  <span className="achievement-date">Desbloqueada em {formatLocalDate(unlock.unlockedOn, { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" })}</span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="achievement-reward">Recompensa: {achievement.rewardXp} XP</span>
                )}
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<Trophy aria-hidden="true" />}
            title="O catálogo oficial ainda não está disponível"
            body="Os critérios e recompensas dependem da especificação original. Nenhuma conquista ou recompensa fictícia foi adicionada."
          />
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export function GoalsPage({
  state,
  goals,
  onCreateGoal,
  onEditGoal,
  onAddContribution,
  onCorrectContribution,
  onArchiveGoal,
}: {
  state: AppState;
  goals: GoalSummary[];
  onCreateGoal: () => void;
  onEditGoal: (goal: GoalSummary) => void;
  onAddContribution: (goal: GoalSummary) => void;
  onCorrectContribution: (goal: GoalSummary, contributionId: string) => void;
  onArchiveGoal: (goal: GoalSummary) => void;
}) {
  const inProgress = goals.filter((summary) => !summary.isComplete);
  const completed = goals.filter((summary) => summary.isComplete);

  return (
    <div className="content fade-up">
      <PageHeading
        eyebrow="Seu futuro, no seu ritmo"
        title="Metas financeiras"
        subtitle="Acompanhe valores e aportes sem misturá-los ao seu progresso em XP."
        action={<button className="button button-primary" onClick={onCreateGoal} data-testid="button-create-goal"><Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Criar meta</button>}
      />
      {!goals.length ? (
        <section className="card">
          <EmptyState
            icon={<CircleDollarSign aria-hidden="true" />}
            title="Dê um propósito a uma meta"
            body="Defina o valor desejado e registre aportes em centavos. O saldo e o percentual serão calculados pelos seus lançamentos."
            action={<button className="button button-primary" onClick={onCreateGoal} data-testid="button-empty-create-goal"><Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Criar primeira meta</button>}
          />
        </section>
      ) : (
        <>
          {inProgress.length > 0 && (
            <section className="goal-section">
              <h2 className="section-title">Em andamento</h2>
              <div className="goal-cards">
                {inProgress.map((summary) => (
                  <GoalCard
                    key={summary.goal.id}
                    state={state}
                    summary={summary}
                    onEdit={() => onEditGoal(summary)}
                    onContribute={() => onAddContribution(summary)}
                    onCorrect={(id) => onCorrectContribution(summary, id)}
                    onArchive={() => onArchiveGoal(summary)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}
          {completed.length > 0 && (
            <section className="goal-section">
              <h2 className="section-title">Concluídas</h2>
              <div className="goal-cards">
                {completed.map((summary) => (
                  <GoalCard
                    key={summary.goal.id}
                    state={state}
                    summary={summary}
                    onEdit={() => onEditGoal(summary)}
                    onContribute={() => onAddContribution(summary)}
                    onCorrect={(id) => onCorrectContribution(summary, id)}
                    onArchive={() => onArchiveGoal(summary)}
                  />
                ))}
              </div>
            </section>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function GoalCard({
  state,
  summary,
  onEdit,
  onContribute,
  onCorrect,
  onArchive,
}: {
  state: AppState;
  summary: GoalSummary;
  onEdit: () => void;
  onContribute: () => void;
  onCorrect: (id: string) => void;
  onArchive: () => void;
}) {
  return (
    <article className="card goal-card" data-testid={`card-goal-${summary.goal.id}`}>
      <div className="goal-card-heading">
        <div>
          <div className="eyebrow">{summary.isComplete ? "Meta concluída" : "Meta em andamento"}</div>
          <h2 className="card-title">{summary.goal.name}</h2>
          {summary.goal.description && <p className="goal-description">{summary.goal.description}</p>}
        </div>
        {summary.isComplete && <StatusLabel tone="complete"><Check aria-hidden="true" /> Concluída</StatusLabel>}
      </div>
      <div className="goal-total">
        {formatMoneyForState(state, summary.contributedCents)}
        <span> de {formatMoneyForState(state, summary.goal.targetCents)}</span>
      </div>
      <div className="goal-progress" role="progressbar" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={summary.percentComplete} aria-label={`${summary.percentComplete}% da meta`}>
        <span style={{ width: `${summary.percentComplete}%` }} />
      </div>
      <div className="goal-progress-caption">
        <span>{summary.percentComplete}% da meta</span>
        <span>{formatMoneyForState(state, summary.remainingCents)} restantes</span>
      </div>
      <div className="goal-actions">
        <button type="button" className="button button-primary" onClick={onContribute} data-testid={`button-add-contribution-${summary.goal.id}`}><Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Registrar aporte</button>
        <button type="button" className="button button-soft" onClick={onEdit} data-testid={`button-edit-goal-${summary.goal.id}`}><Edit3 aria-hidden="true" /> Editar meta</button>
        <button type="button" className="icon-button" onClick={onArchive} aria-label={`Arquivar meta ${summary.goal.name}`} data-testid={`button-archive-goal-${summary.goal.id}`}><Archive aria-hidden="true" /></button>
      </div>
      <div className="goal-contributions">
        <CardHeading title="Movimentações" detail={`${summary.contributions.length} ${summary.contributions.length === 1 ? "aporte" : "aportes"}`} />
        {summary.contributions.length ? (
          <div className="transaction-list">
            {summary.contributions.slice().reverse().map((contribution) => (
              <div className="transaction-row" key={contribution.id} data-testid={`row-contribution-${contribution.id}`}>
                <div className="transaction-copy">
                  <strong>{contribution.note || "Aporte"}</strong>
                  <span>{formatLocalDate(contribution.date, { day: "numeric", month: "short", year: "numeric" })}</span>
                  {contribution.revisions.map((revision, index) => (
                    <small key={`${contribution.id}:${index}`} className="correction-note">
                      Corrigido de {formatMoneyForState(state, revision.previousAmountCents)} · {revision.note || formatLocalDate(revision.correctedAt.slice(0, 10))}
                    </small>
                  ))}
                </div>
                <div className="transaction-end">
                  <div className="transaction-amount">{formatMoneyForState(state, contribution.amountCents)}</div>
                  <button type="button" className="text-action" onClick={() => onCorrect(contribution.id)} data-testid={`button-correct-contribution-${contribution.id}`}>Corrigir</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <div className="mini-empty">Ainda não há aportes nesta meta.</div>
        )}
      </div>
    </article>
  );
}

export function SettingsPage({
  preferences,
  error,
  onUpdate,
  onExport,
  onImport,
  onClearData,
}: {
  preferences: AppPreferences;
  error: string | null;
  onUpdate: (preferences: Partial<AppPreferences>) => void;
  onExport: () => void;
  onImport: (file: File) => Promise<string | null>;
  onClearData: () => void;
}) {
  const [importMessage, setImportMessage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [importError, setImportError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const readBackup = async (file?: File) => {
    setImportMessage(null);
    setImportError(null);
    if (!file) return;
    const result = await onImport(file);
    if (result) {
      setImportError(result);
    } else {
      setImportMessage("Backup validado e restaurado.");
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="content fade-up">
      <PageHeading
        eyebrow="Do seu jeito"
        title="Configurações"
        subtitle="Personalize a aparência e mantenha seus dados sob seu controle."
      />
      <section className="card page-card">
        <CardHeading title="Aparência" detail="Ajuste o aplicativo ao seu conforto" />
        <div className="setting-section">
          <div><h3>Tema</h3><p>Escolha entre o tema claro e o escuro.</p></div>
          <div className="theme-options" role="group" aria-label="Tema">
            {(["light", "dark"] as const).map((theme) => (
              <button
                type="button"
                key={theme}
                onClick={() => onUpdate({ theme })}
                className={`theme-option ${preferences.theme === theme ? "active" : ""}`}
                aria-pressed={preferences.theme === theme}
                data-testid={`button-theme-${theme}`}
              >
                {theme === "light" ? "Claro" : "Escuro"}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
        </div>
        <div className="setting-section">
          <div><h3>Movimento reduzido</h3><p>Reduza animações e transições da interface.</p></div>
          <label className="preference-toggle">
            <input type="checkbox" checked={preferences.reducedMotion} onChange={(event) => onUpdate({ reducedMotion: event.target.checked })} data-testid="input-reduced-motion" />
            <span>{preferences.reducedMotion ? "Ativado" : "Desativado"}</span>
          </label>
        </div>
        <div className="setting-section">
          <div><h3>Início da semana</h3><p>Escolha como o calendário organiza os dias.</p></div>
          <select className="select compact-select" value={preferences.weekStartsOn} onChange={(event) => onUpdate({ weekStartsOn: Number(event.target.value) as 0 | 1 })} aria-label="Início da semana" data-testid="select-week-start">
            <option value={1}>Segunda-feira</option>
            <option value={0}>Domingo</option>
          </select>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="card page-card">
        <CardHeading title="Gerenciamento de dados" detail="Os dados do aplicativo ficam no armazenamento local deste dispositivo" />
        <div className="setting-section">
          <div><h3>Exportar backup</h3><p>Baixe seus hábitos, registros, metas e preferências em JSON.</p></div>
          <button type="button" className="button button-soft" onClick={onExport} data-testid="button-export-backup"><ArrowDownToLine aria-hidden="true" /> Exportar JSON</button>
        </div>
        <div className="setting-section">
          <div><h3>Restaurar backup</h3><p>O arquivo será validado antes de substituir os dados atuais.</p></div>
          <label className="button button-soft" htmlFor="backup-file" data-testid="label-import-backup">
            Escolher arquivo
            <input id="backup-file" type="file" accept="application/json,.json" hidden onChange={(event) => void readBackup(event.target.files?.[0])} data-testid="input-import-backup" />
          </label>
        </div>
        {importMessage && <p className="inline-message success-message" role="status">{importMessage}</p>}
        {(importError || error) && <p className="inline-message error-message" role="alert">{importError || error}</p>}
        <div className="setting-section danger-section">
          <div><h3>Apagar dados deste dispositivo</h3><p>Esta ação remove os dados locais do Passo a Passo após confirmação.</p></div>
          <button type="button" className="button button-danger" onClick={onClearData} data-testid="button-clear-data"><Trash2 aria-hidden="true" /> Apagar dados</button>
        </div>
      </section>

      <section className="card page-card">
        <CardHeading title="Sobre o aplicativo" />
        <div className="setting-section">
          <div><h3>Progresso com intenção</h3><p>O Passo a Passo organiza hábitos, histórico e metas financeiras em um só lugar.</p></div>
          <span className="brand-mark"><Leaf size={18} aria-hidden="true" /></span>
        </div>
        <div className="setting-section">
          <div><h3>Uso offline</h3><p>As funcionalidades essenciais não dependem da API.</p></div>
          <span className="offline-chip"><span className="offline-dot" /> Local</span>
        </div>
        <div className="setting-section">
          <div><h3>Privacidade</h3><p>Seus registros permanecem no armazenamento deste navegador. Exporte um backup antes de trocar de dispositivo.</p></div>
          <ShieldCheck aria-hidden="true" className="setting-icon" />
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}


================================================================
ARQUIVO 85/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/pages/habits-history-pages.tsx
