import { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "wouter";
import {
  CalendarDays,
  Check,
  ChevronLeft,
  ChevronRight,
  Edit3,
  Leaf,
  Pause,
  Play,
  Plus,
  Trash2,
} from "lucide-react";
import { formatLocalDate, formatWeekdays, getHabitHistory, getMonthView } from "@/app/view-models";
import { toLocalDateKey } from "@/domain/dates";
import type { HabitView } from "@/app/view-models";
import type { AppState, HabitStatus } from "@/domain/model";
import { CardHeading, EmptyState, PageHeading, StatusLabel } from "./common";

export function HabitsPage({
  habits,
  onAddHabit,
  onEditHabit,
  onSetStatus,
  onJustify,
  onPauseHabit,
  onDeleteHabit,
}: {
  habits: HabitView[];
  onAddHabit: () => void;
  onEditHabit: (habit: HabitView) => void;
  onSetStatus: (id: string, status: "pending" | "completed" | "neutral", level?: "main" | "min") => void;
  onJustify: (habit: HabitView) => void;
  onPauseHabit: (id: string, paused: boolean) => void;
  onDeleteHabit: (habit: HabitView) => void;
}) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<"all" | "active" | "paused">("all");
  const shown = habits.filter((habit) => filter === "all" || habit.status === filter);

  return (
    <div className="content fade-up">
      <PageHeading
        eyebrow="Sua rotina"
        title="Hábitos"
        subtitle="Crie uma rotina que combine com a sua vida, sem pressa."
        action={
          <button className="button button-primary" onClick={onAddHabit} data-testid="button-add-habit">
            <Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Criar hábito
          </button>
        }
      />
      <section className="card page-card">
        <div className="toolbar">
          <div className="filter-row" role="group" aria-label="Filtrar hábitos">
            {(["all", "active", "paused"] as const).map((item) => (
              <button
                key={item}
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setFilter(item)}
                className={`filter-chip ${filter === item ? "active" : ""}`}
                aria-pressed={filter === item}
                data-testid={`button-filter-${item}`}
              >
                {item === "all" ? `Todos · ${habits.length}` : item === "active" ? "Ativos" : "Pausados"}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <span className="card-kicker">Você pode alterar os dias e a descrição sem perder o histórico.</span>
        </div>
        {shown.length ? (
          <div className="table-list">
            {shown.map((habit) => (
              <div className="manage-row" key={habit.id} data-testid={`row-habit-${habit.id}`}>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className={`check-button ${habit.dayStatus === "completed" ? "done" : ""}`}
                  disabled={!habit.scheduledToday}
                  onClick={() => onSetStatus(habit.id, habit.dayStatus === "completed" ? "pending" : "completed")}
                  aria-label={habit.dayStatus === "completed" ? `Desmarcar ${habit.name}` : `Concluir ${habit.name}`}
                  data-testid={`button-habit-status-${habit.id}`}
                >
                  {habit.dayStatus === "completed" && <Check aria-hidden="true" />}
                </button>
                <div className="manage-copy">
                  <strong>{habit.name}</strong>
                  <span>{habit.description || formatWeekdays(habit.weekdays)} · {habit.status === "active" ? "ativo" : "pausado"}</span>
                </div>
                {habit.scheduledToday && (
                  <StatusLabel tone={habit.dayStatus === "completed" ? "complete" : habit.dayStatus === "justified" ? "justified" : "pending"}>
                    {statusLabel(habit.dayStatus)}
                  </StatusLabel>
                )}
                <Link
                  className="icon-button"
                  href={`/history?habit=${encodeURIComponent(habit.id)}`}
                  title="Ver histórico do hábito"
                  aria-label={`Ver histórico de ${habit.name}`}
                  data-testid={`link-habit-history-${habit.id}`}
                >
                  <CalendarDays aria-hidden="true" />
                </Link>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="icon-button"
                  onClick={() => onEditHabit(habit)}
                  aria-label={`Editar ${habit.name}`}
                  data-testid={`button-edit-habit-${habit.id}`}
                >
                  <Edit3 aria-hidden="true" />
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="icon-button"
                  onClick={() => onPauseHabit(habit.id, habit.status === "active")}
                  aria-label={habit.status === "active" ? `Pausar ${habit.name}` : `Retomar ${habit.name}`}
                  data-testid={`button-pause-habit-${habit.id}`}
                >
                  {habit.status === "active" ? <Pause aria-hidden="true" /> : <Play aria-hidden="true" />}
                </button>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  className="icon-button"
                  onClick={() => onDeleteHabit(habit)}
                  aria-label={`Excluir ${habit.name}`}
                  data-testid={`button-delete-habit-${habit.id}`}
                >
                  <Trash2 aria-hidden="true" />
                </button>
                {habit.scheduledToday && habit.dayStatus !== "justified" && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="text-action"
                    onClick={() => onJustify(habit)}
                    data-testid={`button-justify-habit-${habit.id}`}
                  >
                    Justificar hoje
                  </button>
                )}
                {habit.scheduledToday && habit.dayStatus === "pending" && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="text-action secondary-action"
                    onClick={() => onSetStatus(habit.id, "neutral")}
                    data-testid={`button-neutral-habit-${habit.id}`}
                  >
                    Marcar neutro
                  </button>
                )}
                {habit.scheduledToday && habit.dayStatus === "neutral" && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    className="text-action secondary-action"
                    onClick={() => onSetStatus(habit.id, "pending")}
                    data-testid={`button-reactivate-habit-${habit.id}`}
                  >
                    Reativar
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <EmptyState
            icon={<Leaf aria-hidden="true" />}
            title={filter === "all" ? "Sua lista está pronta" : `Nenhum hábito ${filter === "active" ? "ativo" : "pausado"}`}
            body={filter === "all"
              ? "Adicione um hábito importante para você e escolha os dias que combinam com sua rotina."
              : "A lista será atualizada quando houver hábitos neste filtro."}
            action={filter === "all" ? (
              <button className="button button-primary" onClick={onAddHabit} data-testid="button-empty-add-habit">
                <Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Criar primeiro hábito
              </button>
            ) : undefined}
          />
        )}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

export function HistoryPage({ state }: { state: AppState }) {
  const [month, setMonth] = useState(() => new Date());
  const [selectedHabit, setSelectedHabit] = useState(
    () => new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get("habit") ?? "all",
  );
  const year = month.getFullYear();
  const monthIndex = month.getMonth();
  const monthData = getMonthView(
    state,
    year,
    monthIndex,
    selectedHabit === "all" ? undefined : selectedHabit,
  );
  const monthLabel = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { month: "long", year: "numeric" }).format(month);
  const firstWeekday = (new Date(year, monthIndex, 1).getDay() + 6) % 7;
  const today = toLocalDateKey();
  const selectedHistory = useMemo(() => {
    if (selectedHabit === "all") {
      return state.habitDays
        .map((entry) => {
          const habit = state.habits.find((item) => item.id === entry.habitId);
          const revision = habit?.revisions.find((item) => item.id === entry.habitRevisionId);
          const status = entry.events.at(-1)?.status ?? "pending";
          return {
            date: entry.date,
            name: revision?.name ?? "Hábito",
            status,
            context: entry.events.at(-1)?.context,
          };
        })
        .filter((entry) => entry.date.startsWith(`${year}-${String(monthIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`))
        .sort((a, b) => b.date.localeCompare(a.date));
    }
    return getHabitHistory(state, selectedHabit)
      .filter((entry) => entry.date.startsWith(`${year}-${String(monthIndex + 1).padStart(2, "0")}`));
  }, [state, selectedHabit, year, monthIndex]);

  return (
    <div className="content fade-up">
      <PageHeading
        eyebrow="Seu caminho"
        title="Histórico"
        subtitle="Veja seus hábitos e justificativas sem apagar os passos anteriores."
      />
      <div className="grid dashboard-grid">
        <section className="card">
          <div className="card-title-row">
            <div>
              <h2 className="card-title">Calendário de atividades</h2>
              <div className="card-kicker">Os registros são agrupados pela data local.</div>
            </div>
            <div className="calendar-top">
              <button type="button" className="icon-button" onClick={() => setMonth(new Date(year, monthIndex - 1, 1))} aria-label="Mês anterior" data-testid="button-previous-month"><ChevronLeft aria-hidden="true" /></button>
              <span className="month-title">{monthLabel}</span>
              <button type="button" className="icon-button" onClick={() => setMonth(new Date(year, monthIndex + 1, 1))} aria-label="Próximo mês" data-testid="button-next-month"><ChevronRight aria-hidden="true" /></button>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="field history-filter">
            <label htmlFor="history-habit">Histórico de</label>
            <select className="select" id="history-habit" value={selectedHabit} onChange={(event) => setSelectedHabit(event.target.value)} data-testid="select-history-habit">
              <option value="all">Todos os hábitos</option>
              {state.habits.map((habit) => (
                <option key={habit.id} value={habit.id}>{habit.revisions.at(-1)?.name ?? "Hábito"}</option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
          <div className="calendar-grid" role="grid" aria-label={`Calendário de ${monthLabel}`}>
            {["Seg", "Ter", "Qua", "Qui", "Sex", "Sáb", "Dom"].map((day) => <div className="weekday" key={day} role="columnheader">{day}</div>)}
            {Array.from({ length: firstWeekday }, (_, index) => <div key={`blank-${index}`} aria-hidden="true" />)}
            {monthData.days.map((day) => (
              <div
                className={`calendar-day ${day.date > today ? "muted" : ""} ${day.isComplete ? "calendar-complete" : day.justified > 0 ? "calendar-justified" : day.completed > 0 ? "calendar-partial" : ""}`}
                key={day.date}
                role="gridcell"
                aria-label={`${formatLocalDate(day.date)}: ${day.completed} concluídos, ${day.justified} justificados`}
                data-testid={`calendar-day-${day.date}`}
              >
                <span className="day-number">{Number(day.date.slice(-2))}</span>
                {day.scheduled > 0 && (
                  <span className="day-dot" aria-hidden="true">
                    {day.isComplete ? "✓" : day.completed > 0 ? `${day.completed}/${day.scheduled}` : "•"}
                  </span>
                )}
              </div>
            ))}
          </div>
          <div className="calendar-legend">
            <span><i className="legend-dot complete" />Concluído</span>
            <span><i className="legend-dot partial" />Parcial</span>
            <span><i className="legend-dot justified" />Com justificativa</span>
            <span><i className="legend-dot neutral" />Sem registro</span>
          </div>
        </section>
        <div className="side-stack history-side">
          <section className="card">
            <CardHeading title="Resumo do período" detail={monthLabel} />
            <div className="period-stats">
              <div className="period-stat"><strong>{monthData.completedHabits}</strong><span>hábitos concluídos</span></div>
              <div className="period-stat"><strong>{monthData.justifiedHabits}</strong><span>justificativas</span></div>
              <div className="period-stat"><strong>{monthData.completeDays}</strong><span>dias completos</span></div>
            </div>
          </section>
          <section className="card">
            <CardHeading title="Atividade registrada" detail={selectedHistory.length ? `${selectedHistory.length} registros no período` : "Nenhum registro neste período"} />
            {selectedHistory.length ? (
              <div className="activity-list">
                {selectedHistory.map((entry, index) => (
                  <div className="activity-row" key={`${entry.date}:${entry.name}:${index}`}>
                    <div><strong>{entry.name}</strong><span>{formatLocalDate(entry.date, { day: "numeric", month: "short" })}</span></div>
                    <div className="activity-status"><StatusLabel tone={entry.status}>{statusLabel(entry.status as HabitStatus)}</StatusLabel>{entry.context && <small>{entry.context}</small>}</div>
                  </div>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="mini-empty">As atividades aparecerão aqui conforme forem registradas.</div>
            )}
          </section>
          <section className="card xp-history-card">
            <CardHeading title="Evolução de XP" />
            <div className="mini-empty">
              O histórico de XP será exibido quando a tabela original de progressão estiver disponível.
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function statusLabel(status: HabitStatus): string {
  switch (status) {
    case "completed": return "Concluído";
    case "justified": return "Justificado";
    case "neutral": return "Neutro";
    case "pending": return "Pendente";
  }
}

ARQUIVO 87/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/persistence/local-store.ts
================================================================
import { APP_SCHEMA_VERSION, type AppState } from "../domain/model.ts";
import { createInitialState } from "../domain/state.ts";
import { parseAppState } from "./schema.ts";

export const APP_STORAGE_KEY = "passo-a-passo:state";
export const BACKUP_FORMAT = "passo-a-passo-backup";
export const BACKUP_VERSION = 1;

export interface StoragePort {
  getItem(key: string): string | null;
  setItem(key: string, value: string): void;
  removeItem(key: string): void;
}

interface BackupEnvelope {
  format: typeof BACKUP_FORMAT;
  backupVersion: typeof BACKUP_VERSION;
  exportedAt: string;
  state: AppState;
}

export class LocalStore {
  private readonly storage: StoragePort;
  private readonly key: string;

  constructor(storage: StoragePort, key = APP_STORAGE_KEY) {
    this.storage = storage;
    this.key = key;
  }

  load(): AppState {
    let raw: string | null;
    try {
      raw = this.storage.getItem(this.key);
    } catch (error) {
      throw storageError("Não foi possível acessar os dados deste dispositivo.", error);
    }
    if (raw === null) return createInitialState();
    let decoded: unknown;
    try {
      decoded = JSON.parse(raw);
    } catch (error) {
      throw storageError("Os dados salvos estão corrompidos e não foram alterados.", error);
    }
    return parseAppState(decoded);
  }

  save(state: AppState): void {
    const validated = parseAppState(state);
    try {
      this.storage.setItem(this.key, JSON.stringify(validated));
    } catch (error) {
      throw storageError("Não foi possível salvar. Os dados anteriores foram mantidos.", error);
    }
  }

  exportBackup(state: AppState, exportedAt = new Date()): string {
    const validated = parseAppState(state);
    const backup: BackupEnvelope = {
      format: BACKUP_FORMAT,
      backupVersion: BACKUP_VERSION,
      exportedAt: exportedAt.toISOString(),
      state: validated,
    };
    return JSON.stringify(backup, null, 2);
  }

  importBackup(json: string): AppState {
    let decoded: unknown;
    try {
      decoded = JSON.parse(json);
    } catch (error) {
      throw storageError("O arquivo não contém um JSON válido.", error);
    }
    const backup = parseBackupEnvelope(decoded);
    const restored = parseAppState(backup.state);
    this.save(restored);
    return restored;
  }

  clear(): void {
    try {
      this.storage.removeItem(this.key);
    } catch (error) {
      throw storageError("Não foi possível remover os dados salvos.", error);
    }
  }
}

function parseBackupEnvelope(input: unknown): BackupEnvelope {
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input)) {
    throw new Error("O backup precisa ser um objeto JSON.");
  }
  const candidate = input as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (candidate.format !== BACKUP_FORMAT) {
    throw new Error("Este arquivo não é um backup do Passo a Passo.");
  }
  if (candidate.backupVersion !== BACKUP_VERSION) {
    throw new Error("A versão deste arquivo de backup não é compatível.");
  }
  if (typeof candidate.exportedAt !== "string" || !Number.isFinite(Date.parse(candidate.exportedAt))) {
    throw new Error("A data de exportação do backup é inválida.");
  }
  if (
    !candidate.state ||
    typeof candidate.state !== "object" ||
    (candidate.state as Record<string, unknown>).schemaVersion !== APP_SCHEMA_VERSION
  ) {
    throw new Error("O backup não contém um estado de aplicativo compatível.");
  }
  return {
    format: BACKUP_FORMAT,
    backupVersion: BACKUP_VERSION,
    exportedAt: candidate.exportedAt,
    state: candidate.state as AppState,
  };
}

function storageError(message: string, cause: unknown): Error {
  const detail = cause instanceof Error ? cause.message : String(cause);
  return new Error(`${message} ${detail}`);
}

ARQUIVO 88/206: artifacts/passo-a-passo/src/persistence/schema.ts
================================================================
import { z } from "zod/v4";
import { isLocalDate } from "../domain/dates.ts";
import { APP_SCHEMA_VERSION } from "../domain/model.ts";
import type { AppState } from "../domain/model.ts";

const idSchema = z.string().min(1).max(200);
const dateSchema = z.string().refine(isLocalDate, "Data de calendário inválida.");
const timestampSchema = z.string().refine(
  (value) => Number.isFinite(Date.parse(value)),
  "Data e hora inválidas.",
);
const weekdaySchema = z.number().int().min(0).max(6);

const goalSpecSchema = z.object({
  value: z.number().positive().max(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER),
  unit: z.string().trim().min(1).max(40),
}).strict();

const habitRevisionSchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(80),
  description: z.string().max(500),
  weekdays: z.array(weekdaySchema).min(1).max(7),
  mainGoal: goalSpecSchema.optional(),
  minGoal: goalSpecSchema.optional(),
  effectiveFrom: dateSchema,
}).strict().refine(
  (revision) => new Set(revision.weekdays).size === revision.weekdays.length,
  "Os dias da semana não podem se repetir.",
);

const lifecycleEventSchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  status: z.enum(["active", "paused", "archived"]),
  effectiveFrom: dateSchema,
}).strict();

const habitSchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  createdOn: dateSchema,
  revisions: z.array(habitRevisionSchema).min(1),
  lifecycle: z.array(lifecycleEventSchema).min(1),
}).strict();

const statusEventSchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  status: z.enum(["pending", "completed", "justified", "neutral"]),
  context: z.string().max(2000).optional(),
  level: z.enum(["main", "min"]).optional(),
  recordedOn: dateSchema,
  recordedAt: timestampSchema,
}).strict().refine(
  (event) => event.status !== "justified" || Boolean(event.context?.trim()),
  "Uma justificativa precisa conter contexto.",
).refine(
  (event) => event.status !== "completed" || event.level === undefined || event.level === "main" || event.level === "min",
  "O nível de conclusão é inválido.",
);

const habitDaySchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  habitId: idSchema,
  date: dateSchema,
  habitRevisionId: idSchema,
  events: z.array(statusEventSchema).min(1),
}).strict();

const contributionRevisionSchema = z.object({
  previousAmountCents: z.number().int().positive().max(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER),
  correctedAt: timestampSchema,
  note: z.string().max(500),
}).strict();

const contributionSchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  goalId: idSchema,
  amountCents: z.number().int().positive().max(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER),
  date: dateSchema,
  note: z.string().max(500),
  createdAt: timestampSchema,
  revisions: z.array(contributionRevisionSchema),
}).strict();

const goalSchema = z.object({
  id: idSchema,
  name: z.string().trim().min(1).max(100),
  description: z.string().max(500),
  targetCents: z.number().int().positive().max(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER),
  createdOn: dateSchema,
  archivedOn: dateSchema.optional(),
}).strict();

const achievementUnlockSchema = z.object({
  achievementId: idSchema,
  unlockedOn: dateSchema,
  unlockedAt: timestampSchema,
  rewardXp: z.number().int().nonnegative().max(Number.MAX_SAFE_INTEGER),
}).strict();

export const appStateSchema = z.object({
  schemaVersion: z.literal(APP_SCHEMA_VERSION),
  preferences: z.object({
    theme: z.enum(["light", "dark"]),
    reducedMotion: z.boolean(),
    weekStartsOn: z.union([z.literal(0), z.literal(1)]),
    currency: z.string().regex(/^[A-Z]{3}$/),
  }).strict(),
  habits: z.array(habitSchema),
  habitDays: z.array(habitDaySchema),
  goals: z.array(goalSchema),
  contributions: z.array(contributionSchema),
  achievementUnlocks: z.array(achievementUnlockSchema),
}).strict();

export function parseAppState(input: unknown): AppState {
  const envelope = readVersion(input);
  let value = envelope.value;
  let version = envelope.version;
  while (version < APP_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
    throw new Error(
      `A versão ${version} dos dados não possui uma migração disponível.`,
    );
  }
  if (version > APP_SCHEMA_VERSION) {
    throw new Error(
      `O backup usa uma versão mais nova (${version}) do que este aplicativo suporta.`,
    );
  }
  const parsed = appStateSchema.safeParse(value);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    throw new Error(`Os dados não passaram na validação: ${parsed.error.issues[0]?.message ?? "formato inválido"}`);
  }
  validateRelationships(parsed.data);
  return parsed.data;
}

function readVersion(input: unknown): { value: unknown; version: number } {
  if (!input || typeof input !== "object" || Array.isArray(input)) {
    throw new Error("Os dados precisam ser um objeto JSON.");
  }
  const record = input as Record<string, unknown>;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(record.schemaVersion) || Number(record.schemaVersion) < 1) {
    throw new Error("O backup não contém uma versão de dados válida.");
  }
  return { value: input, version: Number(record.schemaVersion) };
}

function validateRelationships(state: AppState): void {
  assertUnique(state.habits.map((item) => item.id), "hábitos");
  assertUnique(state.goals.map((item) => item.id), "metas");
  assertUnique(state.contributions.map((item) => item.id), "aportes");
  assertUnique(
    state.habitDays.map((item) => `${item.habitId}\u0000${item.date}`),
    "registros diários",
  );
  assertUnique(state.achievementUnlocks.map((item) => item.achievementId), "conquistas desbloqueadas");

  const habits = new Map(state.habits.map((habit) => [habit.id, habit]));
  for (const entry of state.habitDays) {
    const habit = habits.get(entry.habitId);
    if (!habit || !habit.revisions.some((revision) => revision.id === entry.habitRevisionId)) {
      throw new Error("Um registro diário aponta para um hábito ou versão inexistente.");
    }
  }
  const goals = new Map(state.goals.map((goal) => [goal.id, goal]));
  for (const contribution of state.contributions) {
    if (!goals.has(contribution.goalId)) {
      throw new Error("Um aporte aponta para uma meta inexistente.");
    }
  }
}

function assertUnique(values: string[], label: string): void {
  if (new Set(values).size !== values.length) {
    throw new Error(`O backup contém ${label} duplicados.`);
  }
}

import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { build as esbuild } from "esbuild";
import esbuildPluginPino from "esbuild-plugin-pino";
import { rm } from "node:fs/promises";

// Plugins (e.g. 'esbuild-plugin-pino') may use `require` to resolve dependencies
globalThis.require = createRequire(import.meta.url);

const artifactDir = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));

async function buildAll() {
  const distDir = path.resolve(artifactDir, "dist");
  await rm(distDir, { recursive: true, force: true });

  await esbuild({
    entryPoints: [path.resolve(artifactDir, "src/index.ts")],
    platform: "node",
    bundle: true,
    format: "esm",
    outdir: distDir,
    outExtension: { ".js": ".mjs" },
    logLevel: "info",
    // Some packages may not be bundleable, so we externalize them, we can add more here as needed.
    // Some of the packages below may not be imported or installed, but we're adding them in case they are in the future.
    // Examples of unbundleable packages:
    // - uses native modules and loads them dynamically (e.g. sharp)
    // - use path traversal to read files (e.g. @google-cloud/secret-manager loads sibling .proto files)
    external: [
      "*.node",
      "sharp",
      "better-sqlite3",
      "sqlite3",
      "canvas",
      "bcrypt",
      "argon2",
      "fsevents",
      "re2",
      "farmhash",
      "xxhash-addon",
      "bufferutil",
      "utf-8-validate",
      "ssh2",
      "cpu-features",
      "dtrace-provider",
      "isolated-vm",
      "lightningcss",
      "pg-native",
      "oracledb",
      "mongodb-client-encryption",
      "nodemailer",
      "handlebars",
      "knex",
      "typeorm",
      "protobufjs",
      "onnxruntime-node",
      "@tensorflow/*",
      "@prisma/client",
      "@mikro-orm/*",
      "@grpc/*",
      "@swc/*",
      "@aws-sdk/*",
      "@azure/*",
      "@opentelemetry/*",
      "@google-cloud/*",
      "@google/*",
      "googleapis",
      "firebase-admin",
      "@parcel/watcher",
      "@sentry/profiling-node",
      "@tree-sitter/*",
      "aws-sdk",
      "classic-level",
      "dd-trace",
      "ffi-napi",
      "grpc",
      "hiredis",
      "kerberos",
      "leveldown",
      "miniflare",
      "mysql2",
      "newrelic",
      "odbc",
      "piscina",
      "realm",
      "ref-napi",
      "rocksdb",
      "sass-embedded",
      "sequelize",
      "serialport",

      "wrangler",
      "zeromq",
      "zeromq-prebuilt",
      "playwright",

      "puppeteer-core",

    ],
    sourcemap: "linked",
    plugins: [
      // pino relies on workers to handle logging, instead of externalizing it we use a plugin to handle it
      esbuildPluginPino({ transports: ["pino-pretty"] })
    ],
    // Make sure packages that are cjs only (e.g. express) but are bundled continue to work in our esm output file
    banner: {
      js: `import { createRequire as __bannerCrReq } from 'node:module';
import __bannerPath from 'node:path';
import __bannerUrl from 'node:url';

globalThis.require = __bannerCrReq(import.meta.url);
globalThis.__filename = __bannerUrl.fileURLToPath(import.meta.url);
globalThis.__dirname = __bannerPath.dirname(globalThis.__filename);
    `,
    },
  });
}

buildAll().catch((err) => {
  console.error(err);
  process.exit(1);
});

ARQUIVO 92/206: artifacts/api-server/src/app.ts

import express, { type Express } from "express";
import cors from "cors";
import pinoHttp from "pino-http";
import router from "./routes";
import { logger } from "./lib/logger";

const app: Express = express();

app.use(
  pinoHttp({
    logger,
    serializers: {
      req(req) {
        return {
          id: req.id,
          method: req.method,
          url: req.url?.split("?")[0],
        };
      },
      res(res) {
        return {
          statusCode: res.statusCode,
        };
      },
    },
  }),
);
app.use(cors());
app.use(express.json());
app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));

app.use("/api", router);

export default app;

ARQUIVO 93/206: artifacts/api-server/src/index.ts
================================================================
import app from "./app";
import { logger } from "./lib/logger";

const rawPort = process.env["PORT"];

if (!rawPort) {
  throw new Error(
    "PORT environment variable is required but was not provided.",
  );
}

const port = Number(rawPort);

if (Number.isNaN(port) || port <= 0) {
  throw new Error(`Invalid PORT value: "${rawPort}"`);
}

app.listen(port, (err) => {
  if (err) {
    logger.error({ err }, "Error listening on port");
    process.exit(1);
  }

  logger.info({ port }, "Server listening");
});

const isProduction = process.env.NODE_ENV === "production";

export const logger = pino({
  level: process.env.LOG_LEVEL ?? "info",
  redact: [
    "req.headers.authorization",
    "req.headers.cookie",
    "res.headers['set-cookie']",
  ],
  ...(isProduction
    ? {}
    : {
        transport: {
          target: "pino-pretty",
          options: { colorize: true },
        },
      }),
});

<!--
  This file is the entry for ALL routes, including /preview/* canvas iframes.
  Fonts are loaded here as non-blocking <link> tags (not CSS @import, which is render-blocking).
-->
<html lang="en" style="height: 100%">
  <head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8" />
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0, maximum-scale=1" />

    <meta property="og:title" content="Mockup Canvas" />
    <meta property="og:description" content="UI prototyping sandbox with infinite canvas" />
    <meta property="og:type" content="website" />
    <meta name="twitter:card" content="summary_large_image" />
    <meta name="twitter:title" content="Mockup Canvas" />
    <meta name="twitter:description" content="UI prototyping sandbox with infinite canvas" />

    <title>Mockup Canvas</title>
    <link rel="icon" href="data:image/svg+xml,<svg xmlns='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg' viewBox='0 0 100 100'><text y='.9em' font-size='90'>🎨</text></svg>">

ARQUIVO 103/206: artifacts/mockup-sandbox/mockupPreviewPlugin.ts
================================================================
import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from "fs";
import path from "path";
import glob from "fast-glob";
import chokidar from "chokidar";
import type { FSWatcher } from "chokidar";
import type { Plugin } from "vite";

const MOCKUPS_DIR = "src/components/mockups";
const GENERATED_MODULE = "src/.generated/mockup-components.ts";

interface DiscoveredComponent {
  globKey: string;
  importPath: string;
}

export function mockupPreviewPlugin(): Plugin {
  let root = "";
  let currentSource = "";
  let watcher: FSWatcher | null = null;

  function getMockupsAbsDir(): string {
    return path.join(root, MOCKUPS_DIR);
  }

  function getGeneratedModuleAbsPath(): string {
    return path.join(root, GENERATED_MODULE);
  }

  function isMockupFile(absolutePath: string): boolean {
    const rel = path.relative(getMockupsAbsDir(), absolutePath);
    return (
      !rel.startsWith("..") && !path.isAbsolute(rel) && rel.endsWith(".tsx")
    );
  }

  function isPreviewTarget(relativeToMockups: string): boolean {
    return relativeToMockups
      .split(path.sep)
      .every((segment) => !segment.startsWith("_"));
  }

  async function discoverComponents(): Promise<Array<DiscoveredComponent>> {
    const files = await glob(`${MOCKUPS_DIR}/**/*.tsx`, {
      cwd: root,
      ignore: ["**/_*/**", "**/_*.tsx"],
    });

    return files.map((f) => ({
      globKey: "./" + f.slice("src/".length),
      importPath: path.posix.relative("src/.generated", f),
    }));
  }

  function generateSource(components: Array<DiscoveredComponent>): string {
    const entries = components
      .map(
        (c) =>
          `  ${JSON.stringify(c.globKey)}: () => import(${JSON.stringify(c.importPath)})`,
      )
      .join(",\n");

    return [
      "// This file is auto-generated by mockupPreviewPlugin.ts.",
      "type ModuleMap = Record<string, () => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>>;",
      "export const modules: ModuleMap = {",
      entries,
      "};",
      "",
    ].join("\n");
  }

  function shouldAutoRescan(pathname: string): boolean {
    return (
      pathname.includes("/components/mockups/") ||
      pathname.includes("/.generated/mockup-components")
    );
  }

  let refreshInFlight = false;
  let refreshQueued = false;

  async function refresh(): Promise<boolean> {
    if (refreshInFlight) {
      refreshQueued = true;
      return false;
    }

    refreshInFlight = true;
    let changed = false;
    try {
      const components = await discoverComponents();
      const newSource = generateSource(components);
      if (newSource !== currentSource) {
        currentSource = newSource;
        const generatedModuleAbsPath = getGeneratedModuleAbsPath();
        mkdirSync(path.dirname(generatedModuleAbsPath), { recursive: true });
        writeFileSync(generatedModuleAbsPath, currentSource);
        changed = true;
      }
    } finally {
      refreshInFlight = false;
    }

    if (refreshQueued) {
      refreshQueued = false;
      const followUp = await refresh();
      return changed || followUp;
    }

    return changed;
  }

  async function onFileAddedOrRemoved(): Promise<void> {
    await refresh();
  }

  return {
    name: "mockup-preview",
    enforce: "pre",

    configResolved(config) {
      root = config.root;
    },

    async buildStart() {
      await refresh();
    },

    async configureServer(viteServer) {
      await refresh();

      const mockupsAbsDir = getMockupsAbsDir();
      mkdirSync(mockupsAbsDir, { recursive: true });

      watcher = chokidar.watch(mockupsAbsDir, {
        ignoreInitial: true,
        awaitWriteFinish: {
          stabilityThreshold: 100,
          pollInterval: 50,
        },
      });

      watcher.on("add", (file) => {
        if (
          isMockupFile(file) &&
          isPreviewTarget(path.relative(mockupsAbsDir, file))
        ) {
          void onFileAddedOrRemoved();
        }
      });

      watcher.on("unlink", (file) => {
        if (isMockupFile(file)) {
          void onFileAddedOrRemoved();
        }
      });

      viteServer.middlewares.use((req, res, next) => {
        const requestUrl = new URL(req.url ?? "/", "http://127.0.0.1");
        const pathname = requestUrl.pathname;
        const originalEnd = res.end.bind(res);

        res.end = ((...args: Parameters<typeof originalEnd>) => {
          if (res.statusCode === 404 && shouldAutoRescan(pathname)) {
            void refresh();
          }
          return originalEnd(...args);
        }) as typeof res.end;

        next();
      });
    },

    async closeWatcher() {
      if (watcher) {
        await watcher.close();
      }
    },
  };
}

ARQUIVO 106/206: artifacts/mockup-sandbox/src/App.tsx
================================================================
import { useEffect, useState, type ComponentType } from "react";

import { modules as discoveredModules } from "./.generated/mockup-components";

type ModuleMap = Record<string, () => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>>;

function _resolveComponent(
  mod: Record<string, unknown>,
  name: string,
): ComponentType | undefined {
  const fns = Object.values(mod).filter(
    (v) => typeof v === "function",
  ) as ComponentType[];
  return (
    (mod.default as ComponentType) ||
    (mod.Preview as ComponentType) ||
    (mod[name] as ComponentType) ||
    fns[fns.length - 1]
  );
}

function PreviewRenderer({
  componentPath,
  modules,
}: {
  componentPath: string;
  modules: ModuleMap;
}) {
  const [Component, setComponent] = useState<ComponentType | null>(null);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    let cancelled = false;

    setComponent(null);
    setError(null);

    async function loadComponent(): Promise<void> {
      const key = `./components/mockups/${componentPath}.tsx`;
      const loader = modules[key];
      if (!loader) {
        setError(`No component found at ${componentPath}.tsx`);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const mod = await loader();
        if (cancelled) {
          return;
        }
        const name = componentPath.split("/").pop()!;
        const comp = _resolveComponent(mod, name);
        if (!comp) {
          setError(
            `No exported React component found in ${componentPath}.tsx\n\nMake sure the file has at least one exported function component.`,
          );
          return;
        }
        setComponent(() => comp);
      } catch (e) {
        if (cancelled) {
          return;
        }

        const message = e instanceof Error ? e.message : String(e);
        setError(`Failed to load preview.\n${message}`);
      }
    }

    void loadComponent();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [componentPath, modules]);

  if (error) {
    return (
      <pre style={{ color: "red", padding: "2rem", fontFamily: "system-ui" }}>
        {error}
      </pre>
    );
  }

  if (!Component) return null;

  return <Component />;
}

function getBasePath(): string {
  return import.meta.env.BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
}

function getPreviewExamplePath(): string {
  const basePath = getBasePath();
  return `${basePath}/preview/ComponentName`;
}

function Gallery() {
  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen bg-gray-50 flex items-center justify-center p-8">
      <div className="text-center max-w-md">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-semibold text-gray-900 mb-3">
          Component Preview Server
        </h1>
        <p className="text-gray-500 mb-4">
          This server renders individual components for the workspace canvas.
        </p>
        <p className="text-sm text-gray-400">
          Access component previews at{" "}
          <code className="bg-gray-100 px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded text-gray-600">
            {getPreviewExamplePath()}
          </code>
        </p>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function getPreviewPath(): string | null {
  const basePath = getBasePath();
  const { pathname } = window.location;
  const local =
    basePath && pathname.startsWith(basePath)
      ? pathname.slice(basePath.length) || "/"
      : pathname;
  const match = local.match(/^\/preview\/(.+)$/);
  return match ? match[1] : null;
}

function App() {
  const previewPath = getPreviewPath();

  if (previewPath) {
    return (
      <PreviewRenderer
        componentPath={previewPath}
        modules={discoveredModules}
      />
    );
  }

  return <Gallery />;
}

export default App;

import { Award, CalendarDays, CheckSquare, CircleDollarSign, Compass, Leaf, Plus, Settings2, Sparkles, Trophy } from "lucide-react";

const navigation = [

];

function CurrentNavigation() {
  return <>{navigation.map(({ href, label, icon: Icon }) => (
    <a href={href} key={href} className={`nav-link ${href === "/" ? "active" : ""}`}>

    </a>
  ))}</>;
}

function CurrentShell({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {

function CardHeading({ title, detail, action }: { title: string; detail?: string; action?: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="card-title-row"><div><h2 className="card-title">{title}</h2>{detail && <div className="card-kicker">{detail}</div>}</div>{action}</div>;
}

function EmptyState({ icon, title, body, action }: { icon: React.ReactNode; title: string; body: string; action?: React.ReactNode }) {
  return <div className="empty-state"><div className="empty-icon">{icon}</div><h3>{title}</h3><p>{body}</p>{action}</div>;
}

  .journey-brand-mark svg { width: 19px; height: 19px; }
  .journey-brand > span:last-child { display: grid; gap: 4px; }
  .journey-brand strong { color: #205146; font: 800 15px "Manrope", sans-serif; letter-spacing: -.4px; }
  .journey-brand small { color: #80988d; font-size: 8px; letter-spacing: .13em; text-transform: uppercase; }
  .journey-sidebar-caption { padding: 0 12px 9px; color: #91a29a; font-size: 9px; font-weight: 800; letter-spacing: .13em; text-transform: uppercase; }
  .journey-offline { display: flex; align-items: flex-start; gap: 9px; margin-top: auto; padding: 16px 8px 2px; border-top: 1px solid #dfe8dc; }
  .journey-online-dot { width: 7px; height: 7px; margin-top: 4px; border-radius: 50%; background: #68a880; box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px #deefe2; }
  .journey-offline div { display: grid; gap: 4px; }
  .journey-offline strong { color: #58766a; font-size: 9px; }
  .journey-offline span:last-child { color: #91a198; font-size: 8px; line-height: 1.35; }
}

@media (min-width: 960px) {
  .journey-sidebar { width: 242px; padding: 29px 18px 20px; }
  .journey-main { margin-left: 242px; }
  .journey-content { padding: 17px 46px 55px; }

}


================================================================
ARQUIVO 109/206: artifacts/mockup-sandbox/src/components/mockups/passo-a-passo-dashboard/Journey.tsx

import { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  Award,
  Check,
  ChevronRight,
  CircleDollarSign,
  Compass,
  Flame,
  Leaf,
  Plus,
  Sparkles,
  Target,
  Trash2,
  Trophy,
  X,
} from "lucide-react";
import "./_group.css";
import "./Journey.css";

type Habit = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  note: string;
  completed: boolean;
};

type Goal = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  target: number;
  saved: number;
};

type LocalData = { habits: Habit[]; goals: Goal[] };
type DialogState = { kind: "habit" } | { kind: "goal"; goal?: Goal } | null;
const STORAGE_KEY = "passo-a-passo-journey-v1";

function readLocalData(): LocalData {
  try {
    const saved = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!saved) return { habits: [], goals: [] };
    const parsed = JSON.parse(saved) as Partial<LocalData>;
    return {
      habits: Array.isArray(parsed.habits) ? parsed.habits : [],
      goals: Array.isArray(parsed.goals) ? parsed.goals : [],
    };
  } catch {
    return { habits: [], goals: [] };
  }
}

function formatMoney(value: number) {
  return new Intl.NumberFormat("pt-BR", { style: "currency", currency: "BRL" }).format(value);
}

function todayLabel() {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", {
    weekday: "long",
    day: "numeric",
    month: "long",
  }).format(new Date());
}

function greetingLabel() {
  const hour = new Date().getHours();
  return hour < 12 ? "Bom dia" : hour < 18 ? "Boa tarde" : "Boa noite";
}

function Navigation({ mobile = false }: { mobile?: boolean }) {
  const items = [
    { href: "#journey-today", label: "Hoje", icon: Compass },
    { href: "#journey-habits", label: "Hábitos", icon: Leaf },
    { href: "#journey-achievements", label: "Conquistas", icon: Award },
    { href: "#journey-goals", label: "Metas", icon: CircleDollarSign },
  ];
  return (
    <nav className={mobile ? "journey-mobile-nav" : "journey-nav"} aria-label="Navegação principal">
      {items.map(({ href, label, icon: Icon }, index) => (
        <a
          className={`journey-nav-item ${index === 0 ? "is-current" : ""}`}
          href={href}
          key={href}
          aria-current={index === 0 ? "page" : undefined}
        >
          <Icon aria-hidden="true" />
          <span>{label}</span>
        </a>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}

function SectionTitle({
  title,
  note,
  action,
}: {
  title: string;
  note?: string;
  action?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="journey-section-heading">
      <div>
        <h2>{title}</h2>
        {note && <p>{note}</p>}
      </div>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

function EmptyPanel({
  icon,
  title,
  body,
  action,
}: {
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  title: string;
  body: string;
  action?: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="journey-empty">
      <span className="journey-empty-icon">{icon}</span>
      <h3>{title}</h3>
      <p>{body}</p>
      {action}
    </div>
  );
}

export function Journey() {
  const [data, setData] = useState<LocalData>(() => readLocalData());
  const [dialog, setDialog] = useState<DialogState>(null);
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState("");
  const [activeHabit, setActiveHabit] = useState("");
  const noticeTimer = useRef<number | undefined>(undefined);
  const habitNameRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const completed = useMemo(() => data.habits.filter((habit) => habit.completed).length, [data.habits]);
  const completion = data.habits.length ? Math.round((completed / data.habits.length) * 100) : 0;
  const message = data.habits.length === 0
    ? "Seu próximo passo começa do jeito que você escolher."
    : completed === data.habits.length
      ? "Você fez sua parte por hoje. Pequenos passos viram caminhos."
      : completed > 0
        ? "Um passo já aconteceu. O resto pode esperar o seu ritmo."
        : "Escolha um passo pequeno para começar.";

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
    } catch {
      // The prototype continues to work in memory if local storage is unavailable.
    }
  }, [data]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!dialog) return;
    const timer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      if (dialog.kind === "habit") habitNameRef.current?.focus();
      else document.getElementById("journey-goal-name")?.focus();
    }, 40);
    const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (event.key === "Escape") setDialog(null);
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    return () => {
      window.clearTimeout(timer);
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKeyDown);
    };
  }, [dialog]);

  useEffect(() => () => window.clearTimeout(noticeTimer.current), []);

  function showNotice(text: string) {
    setNotice(text);
    window.clearTimeout(noticeTimer.current);
    noticeTimer.current = window.setTimeout(() => setNotice(""), 2800);
  }

  function toggleHabit(id: string) {
    const habit = data.habits.find((item) => item.id === id);
    if (!habit) return;
    const nextCompleted = !habit.completed;
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      habits: current.habits.map((item) => item.id === id ? { ...item, completed: !item.completed } : item),
    }));
    setActiveHabit(id);
    window.setTimeout(() => setActiveHabit(""), 500);
    if (nextCompleted) showNotice("Mais um passo no seu ritmo. Muito bem.");
  }

  function saveHabit(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const formData = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const name = String(formData.get("name") ?? "").trim();
    const note = String(formData.get("note") ?? "").trim();
    if (!name) return;
    setData((current) => ({
      ...current,
      habits: [...current.habits, { id: crypto.randomUUID(), name, note, completed: false }],
    }));
    setDialog(null);
    showNotice("Seu hábito já tem um lugar na jornada.");
  }

  function saveGoal(event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const formData = new FormData(event.currentTarget);
    const name = String(formData.get("name") ?? "").trim();
    const target = Number(formData.get("target"));
    const saved = Number(formData.get("saved") || 0);
    if (!name || !Number.isFinite(target) || target <= 0 || !Number.isFinite(saved) || saved < 0 || saved > target) return;
    const existing = dialog?.kind === "goal" ? dialog.goal : undefined;
    if (existing) {
      setData((current) => ({
        ...current,
        goals: current.goals.map((goal) => goal.id === existing.id ? { ...goal, name, target, saved } : goal),
      }));
      showNotice("Meta atualizada com os valores que você informou.");
    } else {
      setData((current) => ({
        ...current,
        goals: [...current.goals, { id: crypto.randomUUID(), name, target, saved }],
      }));
      showNotice("Sua meta entrou no mapa.");
    }
    setDialog(null);
  }

  function removeHabit(id: string) {
    setData((current) => ({ ...current, habits: current.habits.filter((habit) => habit.id !== id) }));
    showNotice("Hábito removido da sua lista.");
  }

  function removeGoal(id: string) {
    setData((current) => ({ ...current, goals: current.goals.filter((goal) => goal.id !== id) }));
    showNotice("Meta removida.");
  }

  return (
    <div className="journey">
      <aside className="journey-sidebar">
        <a className="journey-brand" href="#journey-today" aria-label="Passo a Passo, início">
          <span className="journey-brand-mark"><Leaf aria-hidden="true" /></span>
          <span><strong>Passo a Passo</strong><small>um dia de cada vez</small></span>
        </a>
        <div className="journey-sidebar-caption">Seu espaço</div>
        <Navigation />
        <div className="journey-offline">
          <span className="journey-online-dot" />
          <div><strong>Seu espaço, sempre por perto</strong><span>Seus dados ficam neste dispositivo.</span></div>
        </div>
      </aside>

      <main className="journey-main" id="journey-today">
        <header className="journey-topbar">
          <div className="journey-breadcrumb"><span>Seu espaço</span><ChevronRight aria-hidden="true" /><strong>Hoje</strong></div>
          <div className="journey-top-date"><span className="journey-date-dot" />{new Intl.DateTimeFormat("pt-BR", { weekday: "short", day: "numeric", month: "short" }).format(new Date())}</div>
        </header>

        <div className="journey-content">
          <section className="journey-welcome">
            <div className="journey-welcome-orbit" aria-hidden="true"><span /><span /><span /></div>
            <div className="journey-welcome-text">
              <div className="journey-eyebrow">{greetingLabel()} <span>·</span> hoje é um bom começo</div>
              <h1>Um passo de<br className="journey-mobile-break" /> cada vez.</h1>
              <p>{message}</p>
            </div>
            <div className="journey-date-card">
              <span>SEU DIA</span>
              <strong>{todayLabel()}</strong>
            </div>
            <div className="journey-sun-disc" aria-hidden="true"><span /></div>
          </section>

          <section className="journey-progress-card" aria-label="Resumo do seu progresso">
            <div className="journey-progress-main">
              <div className="journey-progress-copy">
                <span className="journey-overline">O caminho de hoje</span>
                <strong>{data.habits.length ? `${completed} de ${data.habits.length}` : "Seu primeiro passo"}</strong>
                <span>{data.habits.length ? "hábitos concluídos" : "começa quando você quiser"}</span>
              </div>
              <div className={`journey-progress-ring ${data.habits.length && completion === 100 ? "is-complete" : ""}`} style={{ "--progress": `${completion}%` } as React.CSSProperties}>
                <span>{data.habits.length ? `${completion}%` : <Leaf aria-hidden="true" />}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="journey-progress-track" role="progressbar" aria-label="Progresso dos hábitos de hoje" aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={completion}>
              <span style={{ width: `${completion}%` }} />
            </div>
            <div className="journey-stat-row">
              <div className="journey-stat">
                <span className="journey-stat-icon streak"><Flame aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                <div><small>Sequência</small><strong>—</strong></div>
                <span className="journey-stat-caption">em breve</span>
              </div>
              <div className="journey-stat">
                <span className="journey-stat-icon xp"><Sparkles aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                <div><small>Experiência</small><strong>—</strong></div>
                <span className="journey-stat-caption">regras pendentes</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </section>

          <div className="journey-layout">
            <section className="journey-panel journey-habits-panel" id="journey-habits">
              <SectionTitle
                title="Hábitos de hoje"
                note={data.habits.length ? `${data.habits.length} ${data.habits.length === 1 ? "passo escolhido" : "passos escolhidos"}` : "Sua rotina começa com o que importa para você"}
                action={<button className="journey-add-button" type="button" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "habit" })}><Plus aria-hidden="true" /><span>Novo hábito</span></button>}
              />
              {data.habits.length ? (
                <div className="journey-habit-list">
                  {data.habits.map((habit) => (
                    <article className={`journey-habit ${habit.completed ? "is-done" : ""} ${activeHabit === habit.id ? "just-toggled" : ""}`} key={habit.id}>
                      <button
                        className="journey-check"
                        type="button"
                        aria-label={habit.completed ? `Desmarcar ${habit.name}` : `Concluir ${habit.name}`}
                        aria-pressed={habit.completed}
                        onClick={() => toggleHabit(habit.id)}
                      >
                        {habit.completed && <Check aria-hidden="true" />}
                      </button>
                      <div className="journey-habit-copy">
                        <strong>{habit.name}</strong>
                        <span>{habit.note || (habit.completed ? "Feito no seu tempo" : "Um passo de cada vez")}</span>
                      </div>
                      <span className={`journey-status ${habit.completed ? "done" : ""}`}>{habit.completed ? "Concluído" : "No seu ritmo"}</span>
                      <button className="journey-icon-button" type="button" aria-label={`Remover ${habit.name}`} onClick={() => removeHabit(habit.id)}><Trash2 aria-hidden="true" /></button>
                    </article>
                  ))}
                  <button className="journey-add-inline" type="button" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "habit" })}><Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Mais um hábito</button>
                </div>
              ) : (
                <EmptyPanel
                  icon={<Leaf aria-hidden="true" />}
                  title="Ainda não há hábitos para hoje"
                  body="Escolha uma ação pequena que faça sentido para você. Ela pode ser o começo de uma rotina."
                  action={<button className="journey-primary-button" type="button" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "habit" })}><Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Criar meu primeiro hábito</button>}
                />
              )}
            </section>

            <div className="journey-aside">
              <section className="journey-level-card">
                <div className="journey-level-decoration" aria-hidden="true" />
                <div className="journey-card-topline"><span>Sua jornada</span><span className="journey-level-tag">NÍVEL</span></div>
                <div className="journey-level-number">—</div>
                <div className="journey-level-caption">Cada passo conta. A tabela oficial de níveis ainda está pendente.</div>
                <div className="journey-level-track" aria-hidden="true"><span /></div>
                <div className="journey-level-foot"><span>Progresso da jornada</span><span>aguardando regras</span></div>
              </section>

              <section className="journey-panel" id="journey-achievements">
                <SectionTitle title="Próximas conquistas" note="Um motivo a mais para continuar" />
                <EmptyPanel
                  icon={<Trophy aria-hidden="true" />}
                  title="O mapa ainda está sendo desenhado"
                  body="O catálogo oficial de conquistas ainda não foi disponibilizado."
                />
                <div className="journey-pending-note"><span><Award aria-hidden="true" /></span>Conquistas pendentes do catálogo oficial</div>
              </section>

              <section className="journey-panel journey-goals-panel" id="journey-goals">
                <SectionTitle
                  title="Metas financeiras"
                  note={data.goals.length ? "Seu plano, no seu ritmo" : undefined}
                  action={<button className="journey-round-add" type="button" aria-label="Adicionar meta financeira" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "goal" })}><Plus aria-hidden="true" /></button>}
                />
                {data.goals.length ? (
                  <div className="journey-goal-list">
                    {data.goals.map((goal) => {
                      const percent = Math.min(100, Math.round((goal.saved / goal.target) * 100));
                      return (
                        <article className="journey-goal" key={goal.id}>
                          <div className="journey-goal-icon"><Target aria-hidden="true" /></div>
                          <div className="journey-goal-content">
                            <div className="journey-goal-head"><strong>{goal.name}</strong><span>{percent}%</span></div>
                            <div className="journey-goal-track" role="progressbar" aria-label={`Progresso da meta ${goal.name}`} aria-valuemin={0} aria-valuemax={100} aria-valuenow={percent}><span style={{ width: `${percent}%` }} /></div>
                            <div className="journey-goal-values">{formatMoney(goal.saved)} <span>de {formatMoney(goal.target)}</span></div>
                          </div>
                          <div className="journey-goal-actions">
                            <button type="button" aria-label={`Editar meta ${goal.name}`} onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "goal", goal })}>Editar</button>
                            <button type="button" aria-label={`Remover meta ${goal.name}`} onClick={() => removeGoal(goal.id)}><Trash2 aria-hidden="true" /></button>
                          </div>
                        </article>
                      );
                    })}
                    <button className="journey-goal-add-link" type="button" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "goal" })}><Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Adicionar meta</button>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="journey-goal-empty">
                    <span className="journey-money-icon"><CircleDollarSign aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                    <div><strong>Um objetivo de cada vez</strong><p>Uma meta pode ajudar a acompanhar seus próximos passos.</p></div>
                    <button className="journey-goal-create" type="button" onClick={() => setDialog({ kind: "goal" })}>Criar meta <ChevronRight aria-hidden="true" /></button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </section>

              <div className="journey-message"><span className="journey-message-icon"><Sparkles aria-hidden="true" /></span><p>{message}</p><span className="journey-message-dash" aria-hidden="true" /></div>
            </div>
          </div>
          <footer className="journey-footer"><span>Um dia de cada vez.</span><span>Feito para seguir no seu ritmo.</span></footer>
        </div>
      </main>

      <Navigation mobile />

      {notice && <div className="journey-toast" role="status"><Sparkles aria-hidden="true" />{notice}</div>}

      {dialog && (
        <div className="journey-modal-backdrop" onMouseDown={(event) => { if (event.target === event.currentTarget) setDialog(null); }}>
          <section className="journey-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="journey-modal-title">
            <button className="journey-modal-close" type="button" onClick={() => setDialog(null)} aria-label="Fechar"><X aria-hidden="true" /></button>
            {dialog.kind === "habit" ? (
              <>
                <span className="journey-modal-icon"><Leaf aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                <h2 id="journey-modal-title">Qual é o seu próximo passo?</h2>
                <p>Um hábito simples já pode abrir caminho. Você pode mudar isso quando quiser.</p>
                <form onSubmit={saveHabit}>
                  <label htmlFor="journey-habit-name">Nome do hábito</label>
                  <input ref={habitNameRef} id="journey-habit-name" name="name" placeholder="Ex.: caminhar um pouco" maxLength={60} required />
                  <label htmlFor="journey-habit-note">Lembrete ou detalhe <span>opcional</span></label>
                  <input id="journey-habit-note" name="note" placeholder="Ex.: depois do café da manhã" maxLength={80} />
                  <div className="journey-modal-actions">
                    <button className="journey-cancel-button" type="button" onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>Agora não</button>
                    <button className="journey-primary-button" type="submit"><Plus aria-hidden="true" /> Adicionar hábito</button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <span className="journey-modal-icon finance"><Target aria-hidden="true" /></span>
                <h2 id="journey-modal-title">{dialog.goal ? "Ajuste sua meta" : "Uma meta com a sua cara"}</h2>
                <p>Informe os valores que fazem sentido para o seu plano. Nada é preenchido por você.</p>
                <form onSubmit={saveGoal}>
                  <label htmlFor="journey-goal-name">Nome da meta</label>
                  <input id="journey-goal-name" name="name" placeholder="Ex.: minha próxima viagem" maxLength={60} defaultValue={dialog.goal?.name ?? ""} required />
                  <div className="journey-form-split">
                    <div><label htmlFor="journey-goal-target">Valor desejado</label><input id="journey-goal-target" name="target" type="number" min="0.01" step="0.01" inputMode="decimal" placeholder="0,00" defaultValue={dialog.goal?.target ?? ""} required /></div>
                    <div><label htmlFor="journey-goal-saved">Já guardei <span>opcional</span></label><input id="journey-goal-saved" name="saved" type="number" min="0" step="0.01" inputMode="decimal" placeholder="0,00" defaultValue={dialog.goal?.saved ?? ""} /></div>
                  </div>
                  <div className="journey-modal-actions">
                    <button className="journey-cancel-button" type="button" onClick={() => setDialog(null)}>Agora não</button>
                    <button className="journey-primary-button" type="submit">{dialog.goal ? "Salvar alterações" : "Criar meta"}</button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </>
            )}
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

ARQUIVO 110/206: artifacts/mockup-sandbox/src/components/mockups/passo-a-passo-dashboard/_group.css

@import url("https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=DM+Sans:wght@400;500;600;700&family=Manrope:wght@500;600;700;800&display=swap");

:root {
  --background: 42 38% 96%;
  --foreground: 166 36% 16%;
  --border: 152 20% 86%;
  --card: 0 0% 100%;
  --card-foreground: 166 36% 16%;
  --sidebar: 160 40% 96%;
  --sidebar-foreground: 166 36% 16%;
  --sidebar-border: 152 20% 86%;
  --primary: 158 47% 34%;
  --secondary: 193 65% 92%;
  --muted: 40 30% 92%;
  --muted-foreground: 167 13% 43%;

import * as React from "react"
import * as AccordionPrimitive from "@radix-ui/react-accordion"
import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const Accordion = AccordionPrimitive.Root

const AccordionItem = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Item>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Item>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AccordionPrimitive.Item
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("border-b", className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
AccordionItem.displayName = "AccordionItem"

const AccordionTrigger = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Trigger>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Trigger>
>(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AccordionPrimitive.Header className="flex">
    <AccordionPrimitive.Trigger
      ref={ref}
      className={cn(
        "flex flex-1 items-center justify-between py-4 text-sm font-medium transition-all hover:underline text-left [&[data-state=open]>svg]:rotate-180",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    >
      {children}
      <ChevronDown className="h-4 w-4 shrink-0 text-muted-foreground transition-transform duration-200" />
    </AccordionPrimitive.Trigger>
  </AccordionPrimitive.Header>
))
AccordionTrigger.displayName = AccordionPrimitive.Trigger.displayName

const AccordionContent = React.forwardRef<
  React.ElementRef<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Content>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof AccordionPrimitive.Content>
>(({ className, children, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AccordionPrimitive.Content
    ref={ref}
    className="overflow-hidden text-sm data-[state=closed]:animate-accordion-up data-[state=open]:animate-accordion-down"
    {...props}
  >
    <div className={cn("pb-4 pt-0", className)}>{children}</div>
  </AccordionPrimitive.Content>
))
AccordionContent.displayName = AccordionPrimitive.Content.displayName

export { Accordion, AccordionItem, AccordionTrigger, AccordionContent }

import * as React from "react"

const AlertDialog = AlertDialogPrimitive.Root

  React.ElementRef<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay>,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<typeof AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay
    className={cn(
      "fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-black/80 data-[state=open]:animate-in data-[state=closed]:animate-out data-[state=closed]:fade-out-0 data-[state=open]:fade-in-0",
      className
    )}
    {...props}
    ref={ref}
  />
))
AlertDialogOverlay.displayName = AlertDialogPrimitive.Overlay.displayName

ARQUIVO 113/206: artifacts/mockup-sandbox/src/components/ui/alert.tsx
================================================================
import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const alertVariants = cva(
  "relative w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 text-sm [&>svg+div]:translate-y-[-3px] [&>svg]:absolute [&>svg]:left-4 [&>svg]:top-4 [&>svg]:text-foreground [&>svg~*]:pl-7",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default: "bg-background text-foreground",
        destructive:
          "border-destructive/50 text-destructive dark:border-destructive [&>svg]:text-destructive",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

const Alert = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLDivElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement> & VariantProps<typeof alertVariants>
>(({ className, variant, ...props }, ref) => (
  <div
    ref={ref}
    role="alert"
    className={cn(alertVariants({ variant }), className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
Alert.displayName = "Alert"

const AlertTitle = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLParagraphElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLHeadingElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <h5
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("mb-1 font-medium leading-none tracking-tight", className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
AlertTitle.displayName = "AlertTitle"

const AlertDescription = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLParagraphElement,
  React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLParagraphElement>
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <div
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("text-sm [&_p]:leading-relaxed", className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
AlertDescription.displayName = "AlertDescription"

export { Alert, AlertTitle, AlertDescription }

ARQUIVO 116/206: artifacts/mockup-sandbox/src/components/ui/badge.tsx
================================================================
import * as React from "react"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const badgeVariants = cva(
  "whitespace-nowrap inline-flex items-center rounded-md border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-ring focus:ring-offset-2" +
  " hover-elevate ",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "border-transparent bg-primary text-primary-foreground shadow-xs",
        secondary:
          "border-transparent bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground",
        destructive:
          "border-transparent bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-xs",
        outline: "text-foreground border [border-color:var(--badge-outline)]",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
    },
  }
)

export interface BadgeProps
  extends React.HTMLAttributes<HTMLDivElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof badgeVariants> {}

function Badge({ className, variant, ...props }: BadgeProps) {
  return (
    <div className={cn(badgeVariants({ variant }), className)} {...props} />
  )
}

export { Badge, badgeVariants }

ARQUIVO 117/206: artifacts/mockup-sandbox/src/components/ui/breadcrumb.tsx

import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { ChevronRight, MoreHorizontal } from "lucide-react"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const Breadcrumb = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLElement,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"nav"> & {
    separator?: React.ReactNode
  }
>(({ ...props }, ref) => <nav ref={ref} aria-label="breadcrumb" {...props} />)
Breadcrumb.displayName = "Breadcrumb"

const BreadcrumbList = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLOListElement,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"ol">
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <ol
    ref={ref}
    className={cn(
      "flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5 break-words text-sm text-muted-foreground sm:gap-2.5",
      className
    )}
    {...props}
  />
))
BreadcrumbList.displayName = "BreadcrumbList"

const BreadcrumbItem = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLLIElement,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"li">
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <li
    ref={ref}
    className={cn("inline-flex items-center gap-1.5", className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
BreadcrumbItem.displayName = "BreadcrumbItem"

const BreadcrumbLink = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLAnchorElement,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"a"> & {
    asChild?: boolean
  }
>(({ asChild, className, ...props }, ref) => {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "a"

  return (
    <Comp
      ref={ref}
      className={cn("transition-colors hover:text-foreground", className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
})
BreadcrumbLink.displayName = "BreadcrumbLink"

const BreadcrumbPage = React.forwardRef<
  HTMLSpanElement,
  React.ComponentPropsWithoutRef<"span">
>(({ className, ...props }, ref) => (
  <span
    ref={ref}
    role="link"
    aria-disabled="true"
    aria-current="page"
    className={cn("font-normal text-foreground", className)}
    {...props}
  />
))
BreadcrumbPage.displayName = "BreadcrumbPage"

const BreadcrumbSeparator = ({
  children,
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"li">) => (
  <li
    role="presentation"
    aria-hidden="true"
    className={cn("[&>svg]:w-3.5 [&>svg]:h-3.5", className)}
    {...props}
  >
    {children ?? <ChevronRight />}
  </li>
)
BreadcrumbSeparator.displayName = "BreadcrumbSeparator"

const BreadcrumbEllipsis = ({
  className,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"span">) => (
  <span
    role="presentation"
    aria-hidden="true"
    className={cn("flex h-9 w-9 items-center justify-center", className)}
    {...props}
  >
    <MoreHorizontal className="h-4 w-4" />
    <span className="sr-only">More</span>
  </span>
)
BreadcrumbEllipsis.displayName = "BreadcrumbElipssis"

export {
  Breadcrumb,
  BreadcrumbList,
  BreadcrumbItem,

import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"
import { Separator } from "@/components/ui/separator"

const buttonGroupVariants = cva(
  "flex w-fit items-stretch has-[>[data-slot=button-group]]:gap-2 [&>*]:focus-visible:relative [&>*]:focus-visible:z-10 has-[select[aria-hidden=true]:last-child]:[&>[data-slot=select-trigger]:last-of-type]:rounded-r-md [&>[data-slot=select-trigger]:not([class*='w-'])]:w-fit [&>input]:flex-1",
  {
    variants: {
      orientation: {
        horizontal:
          "[&>*:not(:first-child)]:rounded-l-none [&>*:not(:first-child)]:border-l-0 [&>*:not(:last-child)]:rounded-r-none",
        vertical:
          "flex-col [&>*:not(:first-child)]:rounded-t-none [&>*:not(:first-child)]:border-t-0 [&>*:not(:last-child)]:rounded-b-none",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      orientation: "horizontal",
    },
  }
)

function ButtonGroup({
  className,
  orientation,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & VariantProps<typeof buttonGroupVariants>) {
  return (
    <div
      role="group"
      data-slot="button-group"
      data-orientation={orientation}
      className={cn(buttonGroupVariants({ orientation }), className)}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function ButtonGroupText({
  className,
  asChild = false,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<"div"> & {
  asChild?: boolean
}) {
  const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "div"

  return (
    <Comp
      className={cn(
        "bg-muted shadow-xs flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border px-4 text-sm font-medium [&_svg:not([class*='size-'])]:size-4 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

function ButtonGroupSeparator({
  className,
  orientation = "vertical",
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof Separator>) {
  return (
    <Separator
      data-slot="button-group-separator"
      orientation={orientation}
      className={cn(
        "bg-input relative !m-0 self-stretch data-[orientation=vertical]:h-auto",
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export {
  ButtonGroup,
  ButtonGroupSeparator,
  ButtonGroupText,
  buttonGroupVariants,
}

ARQUIVO 119/206: artifacts/mockup-sandbox/src/components/ui/button.tsx

import * as React from "react"
import { Slot } from "@radix-ui/react-slot"
import { cva, type VariantProps } from "class-variance-authority"

import { cn } from "@/lib/utils"

const buttonVariants = cva(
  "inline-flex items-center justify-center gap-2 whitespace-nowrap rounded-md text-sm font-medium transition-colors focus-visible:outline-none focus-visible:ring-1 focus-visible:ring-ring disabled:pointer-events-none disabled:opacity-50 [&_svg]:pointer-events-none [&_svg]:size-4 [&_svg]:shrink-0" +
" hover-elevate active-elevate-2",
  {
    variants: {
      variant: {
        default:
          "bg-primary text-primary-foreground border border-primary-border",
        destructive:
          "bg-destructive text-destructive-foreground shadow-sm border-destructive-border",
        outline:
          "border [border-color:var(--button-outline)] shadow-xs active:shadow-none",
        secondary:
          "border bg-secondary text-secondary-foreground border border-secondary-border",
        ghost: "border border-transparent",
        link: "text-primary underline-offset-4 hover:underline",
      },
      size: {
        default: "min-h-9 px-4 py-2",
        sm: "min-h-8 rounded-md px-3 text-xs",
        lg: "min-h-10 rounded-md px-8",
        icon: "h-9 w-9",
      },
    },
    defaultVariants: {
      variant: "default",
      size: "default",
    },
  }
)

export interface ButtonProps
  extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement>,
    VariantProps<typeof buttonVariants> {
  asChild?: boolean
}

const Button = React.forwardRef<HTMLButtonElement, ButtonProps>(
  ({ className, variant, size, asChild = false, ...props }, ref) => {
    const Comp = asChild ? Slot : "button"
    return (
      <Comp
        className={cn(buttonVariants({ variant, size, className }))}
        ref={ref}
        {...props}
      />
    )
  }
)
Button.displayName = "Button"

export { Button, buttonVariants }

        ),

          defaultClassNames.day

          )

        DayButton: CalendarDayButton,

          return (

              <div className="flex size-[--cell-size] items-center justify-center text-center">
                {children}
              </div>

      {...props}

  )

function CalendarDayButton({
  className,
  day,
  modifiers,
  ...props
}: React.ComponentProps<typeof DayButton>) {
  const defaultClassNames = getDefaultClassNames()

  const ref = React.useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null)
  React.useEffect(() => {
    if (modifiers.focused) ref.current?.focus()
  }, [modifiers.focused])

  return (
    <Button
      ref={ref}
      variant="ghost"
      size="icon"
      data-day={day.date.toLocaleDateString()}
      data-selected-single={
        modifiers.selected &&
        !modifiers.range_start &&
        !modifiers.range_end &&
        !modifiers.range_middle
      }
      data-range-start={modifiers.range_start}
      data-range-end={modifiers.range_end}
      data-range-middle={modifiers.range_middle}
      className={cn(
        "data-[selected-single=true]:bg-primary data-[selected-single=true]:text-primary-foreground data-[range-middle=true]:bg-accent data-[range-middle=true]:text-accent-foreground data-[range-start=true]:bg-primary data-[range-start=true]:text-primary-foreground data-[range-end=true]:bg-primary data-[range-end=true]:text-primary-foreground group-data-[focused=true]/day:border-ring group-data-[focused=true]/day:ring-ring/50 flex aspect-square h-auto w-full min-w-[--cell-size] flex-col gap-1 font-normal leading-none data-[range-end=true]:rounded-md data-[range-middle=true]:rounded-none data-[range-start=true]:rounded-md group-data-[focused=true]/day:relative group-data-[focused=true]/day:z-10 group-data-[focused=true]/day:ring-[3px] [&>span]:text-xs [&>span]:opacity-70",
        defaultClassNames.day,
        className
      )}
      {...props}
    />
  )
}

export { Calendar, CalendarDayButton }
